  31  class Offers(unittest.TestCase):
  32      def test_180_offers_in_fixed_cycled_order(self):
  33          m = mission()
  34          for t in range(0, 2000):
  35              m.offer_due(float(t))
  36          ids = [e['id'] for e in m.events]
  37          self.assertEqual(len(ids), 180)
  38          self.assertEqual(ids[:41], list(range(1, 41)) + [1])
  39          self.assertEqual([e['due'] for e in m.events][-1], 1790)

  42          m = mission(work_s=b5.SMOKE_S)

  44          self.assertEqual((b5.SMOKE_S, len(m.events)), (180, 18))

  46      def test_queue_of_one_drops_the_older(self):
  47          m = mission('B')
  48          m.offer({'source': 'scripted', 'id': 1}, 1.0)
  49          m.offer({'source': 'real', 'id': 'R1'}, 2.0)
  50          self.assertEqual([e['fate'] for e in m.events], ['dropped', 'pending'])
  51          ev, _ = m.start(2.0, 30.0)
  52          self.assertEqual(ev['id'], 'R1')
  53          m.close()
  54          self.assertEqual(m.events[1]['fate'], 'deciding')

  57  class Schedulers(unittest.TestCase):
  58      def test_a_decides_only_at_30_s_marks(self):
  59          m = mission('A')
  60          m.offer_due(0.0)
  61          self.assertEqual(m.start(0.0, 30.0)[0]['id'], 1)
  62          m.done(m.events[0], {'top': 'explore'}, 4.0)
  63          m.offer_due(10.0)
  64          self.assertIsNone(m.start(10.0, 30.0))  # pending, but no mark
  65          m.offer_due(20.0)
  66          self.assertIsNone(m.start(29.9, 30.0))
  67          m.offer_due(30.0)
  68          ev, _ = m.start(30.05, 30.0)
  69          self.assertEqual(ev['id'], 4)  # 2 and 3 were replaced
  70          self.assertEqual([e['fate'] for e in m.events[1:3]], ['dropped', 'dropped'])
  71          self.assertIsNone(m.start(60.0, 30.0))  # busy at the mark
  72          m.done(ev, {'top': 'wait'}, 61.0)
  73          self.assertEqual(m.ticks, {'decided': 2, 'empty': 0, 'paused': 0, 'busy': 1})
  74  
  75      def test_a_tick_in_pause_keeps_the_event_pending(self):

  77          m.offer_due(0.0)
  78          m.set_ok(False)
  79          self.assertIsNone(m.start(0.0, 30.0))
  80          self.assertEqual(m.pending['id'], 1)
  81          m.set_ok(True)
  82          self.assertIsNone(m.start(5.0, 30.0))  # waits for the next mark
  83          self.assertEqual(m.start(30.0, 30.0)[0]['id'], 1)
  84          self.assertEqual(m.ticks['paused'], 1)

  95          self.assertIsNone(m.start(0.0, None))
  96          m.set_ok(False)
  97          self.assertIsNone(m.start(0.1, 35.9))
  98          m.set_ok(True)
  99          ev, gen = m.start(0.2, 35.9)
 100          self.assertEqual(ev['id'], 1)
 101          m.offer_due(10.0)
 102          self.assertIsNone(m.start(10.0, 30.0))  # previous decision not finished
 103          m.done(ev, {'top': 'explore'}, 10.5)

 114  class Cancel(unittest.TestCase):
 115      def test_forbid_cancels_for_good_and_requeues(self):
 116          m = mission('B')
 117          m.offer({'source': 'scripted', 'id': 1}, 0.0)
 118          ev, gen = m.start(0.0, 30.0)
 119          self.assertTrue(m.allowed(gen))
 120          m.set_ok(False)
 121          m.set_ok(True)
 122          self.assertFalse(m.allowed(gen))  # a later allow does not revive it
 123          m.done(ev, {'cancelled': True}, 2.0)
 124          self.assertIs(m.pending, ev)
 125          ev2, gen2 = m.start(40.0, 30.0)
 126          m.done(ev2, {'top': 'look'}, 44.0)
 127          self.assertEqual((ev['fate'], ev['latency_s'], ev['wait_s'], ev['attempts']), ('served', 44.0, 40.0, 2))
 128  
 129      def test_cancelled_with_a_newer_event_pending(self):
 130          m = mission('B')
 131          m.offer({'source': 'scripted', 'id': 1}, 0.0)
 132          ev, _ = m.start(0.0, 30.0)
 133          m.offer({'source': 'real', 'id': 'R1'}, 1.0)
 134          m.done(ev, {'cancelled': True}, 2.0)
 135          self.assertEqual((ev['fate'], m.pending['id']), ('cancelled', 'R1'))

 137      def test_latency_and_errors(self):
 138          m = mission('B', t0=100.0)
 139          m.offer({'source': 'scripted', 'id': 1}, 105.0)
 140          ev, _ = m.start(107.0, 30.0)
 141          m.done(ev, {'top': 'wait'}, 111.0)
 142          self.assertEqual((ev['offered'], ev['started'], ev['ended'], ev['latency_s'], ev['wait_s']),
 143                           (5.0, 7.0, 11.0, 6.0, 2.0))
 144          m.offer({'source': 'scripted', 'id': 2}, 112.0)
 145          ev, _ = m.start(112.0, 30.0)
 146          m.done(ev, {'error': 'x'}, 113.0)
 147          self.assertEqual(ev['fate'], 'error')

 154          b4.slot = lambda action: self.calls.append(action) or {'action': action, 'wall_s': 0.01}

 157              post('/completion', {})
 158              post('/completion', {})
 159              return {'top': 'explore', 'reason': 'r', 'reason_tokens': 3}
 160          b5.b2.score = score
 161          b5.b2.preflight = lambda post, s, blocks, info, tag: {('V3', 'FWD', it['id']): ('t', [1], {})

 170      def ev(self, n, it):
 171          return {'source': 'scripted' if isinstance(it['id'], int) else 'real', 'item': it, 'n': n}
 172  
 173      def test_restore_every_5th_and_real_render(self):
 174          prompts = {('V3', 'FWD', 1): ('t', [1], {})}
 175          r = b5.decide(self.ev(5, ITEMS[0]), lambda: True, prompts, {'missions': {}})
 176          self.assertEqual((r['top'], self.calls), ('explore', ['restore']))
 177          r = b5.decide(self.ev(6, b5.real_item(1, 'A person appeared far away.')), lambda: True, prompts,
 178                        {'missions': {}})

 182      def flip_on_final(self, action):
 183          """b4.post that runs action() while the second (final) /completion is in flight."""
 184          n = []
 185  
 186          def post(path, body):
 187              if path == '/completion':
 188                  n.append(1)
 189                  if len(n) == 2:
 190                      action()
 191              return {'timings': {'prompt_n': 5}}
 192          b4.post = post
 193  
 194      def test_forbid_during_the_final_completion_cancels(self):
 195          allowed = [True]
 196          self.flip_on_final(lambda: allowed.__setitem__(0, False))
 197          r = b5.decide(self.ev(1, ITEMS[0]), lambda: allowed[0], {('V3', 'FWD', 1): ('t', [1], {})}, {})
 198          self.assertTrue(r['cancelled'])
 199          self.assertNotIn('top', r)
 200  
 201      def test_forbid_then_allow_before_the_response_still_cancels(self):
 202          m = mission('B')
 203          m.offer({'source': 'scripted', 'id': 1, 'item': ITEMS[0]}, 0.0)
 204          ev, gen = m.start(0.0, 30.0)
 205          self.flip_on_final(lambda: (m.set_ok(False), m.set_ok(True)))
 206          r = b5.decide(ev, lambda: m.allowed(gen), {('V3', 'FWD', 1): ('t', [1], {})}, {})
 207          self.assertTrue(r['cancelled'])
 208          m.done(ev, r, 5.0)
 209          self.assertIs(m.pending, ev)  # the existing requeue rule
 210  
 211      def test_forbid_after_the_last_response_while_scoring_cancels(self):
 212          allowed = [True]
 213  
 214          def score(post, it, v, order, prompt):  # b2.score's own work after its last request
 215              post('/completion', {})
 216              post('/completion', {})
 217              allowed[0] = False
 218              return {'top': 'explore', 'reason': 'r', 'reason_tokens': 3}
 219          b5.b2.score = score
 220          r = b5.decide(self.ev(1, ITEMS[0]), lambda: allowed[0], {('V3', 'FWD', 1): ('t', [1], {})}, {})
 221          self.assertTrue(r['cancelled'])
 222  
 223      def test_forbid_during_the_restore_cancels(self):
 224          allowed = [True]
 225          b4.slot = lambda action: allowed.__setitem__(0, False) or {'action': action, 'wall_s': 0.01}
 226          r = b5.decide(self.ev(5, ITEMS[0]), lambda: allowed[0], {('V3', 'FWD', 1): ('t', [1], {})}, {})
 227          self.assertTrue(r['cancelled'])
 228  
 229      def test_forbidden_before_a_request_is_cancelled(self):
 230          prompts = {('V3', 'FWD', 1): ('t', [1], {})}
 231          r = b5.decide(self.ev(1, ITEMS[0]), lambda: False, prompts, {})
 232          self.assertTrue(r['cancelled'])
 233          self.assertNotIn('error', r)
 234          r = b5.decide(self.ev(5, ITEMS[0]), lambda: False, prompts, {})
 235          self.assertTrue(r['cancelled'])
 236          self.assertEqual(self.calls, [])

 241  class FakeHeat:
 242      """Scripted heat.decide() targets by fake time: [(from_t, target)]; status by time too."""
 243  
 244      def __init__(self, clock, plan, status=()):
 245          self.clock, self.plan, self.status, self.phase, self.since = clock, plan, list(status), 'ACTIVE', clock()
 246          self.events, self.monitor = [], self
 247  
 248      def decide(self, distance):
 249          target = [t for at, t in self.plan if self.clock() >= at][-1]
 250          return target, f'plan {target}'
 251  
 252      def readings(self):
 253          st = [s for at, s in self.status if self.clock() >= at]
 254          return {'dump': {'status': st[-1] if st else 0, 'skin': 30.0}, 'fast': []}
 255  
 256      def enter(self, phase, since=None):
 257          self.phase = phase
 258  
 259      def now(self):
 260          return self.clock()
 261  
 262      def event(self, name, detail=None):
 263          self.events.append(name)
 264  
 265  
 266  class Image:
 267      size, height = (640, 480), 480
 268  
 269  
 270  class FakeRobot:
 271      def __init__(self, clock, persons=None, fail_start=False, start_s=0.0):
 272          self.clock, self.persons, self.fail_start, self.start_s = clock, persons or {}, fail_start, start_s
 273          self.camera_session, self.capturing, self.starts, self.stops, self.frame = 's', True, 0, 0, 0
 274          self.detector, self.size_policy = self, self
 275  
 276      def start_camera(self, heat=None):
 277          self.starts += 1
 278          self.capturing = True
 279          self.clock.sleep(self.start_s)  # a restart takes time
 280          if self.fail_start:
 281              raise RuntimeError('RobotCam did not publish a usable frame')
 282  
 283      def stop_camera(self):
 284          self.stops += 1
 285          self.capturing = False
 286          if getattr(self, 'm', None):
 287              self.ok_at_stop = self.m.ok
 288  
 289      def read(self, **kw):
 290          if not self.capturing:
 291              return {'status': 'missing'}
 292          self.frame += 1
 293          return {'status': 'ok', 'session': 's', 'frame': self.frame, 'age_s': 0.5, 'image': Image()}
 294  
 295      def next_size(self):
 296          return 640 if self.frame % 5 == 1 else 320
 297  
 298      def observe(self, dets, h):
 299          pass
 300  
 301      def detect(self, image, size):
 302          self.clock.sleep(0.2)
 303          area = [b for at, b in self.persons.items() if self.clock() >= at]
 304          side = {None: 0, 'far': 50, 'medium': 200, 'close': 300, 'very close': 500}[area[-1] if area else None]
 305          return [{'class_name': 'person', 'conf': 0.9, 'box_xyxy': (0, 0, side, side)}] if side else []
 306  
 307      def affinity(self):
 308          return {1: [4, 5]}
 309  
 310      def config(self):
 311          return {}
 312  
 313  
 314  class Loop(unittest.TestCase):
 315      def setUp(self):
 316          self.c = test_b4.FakeClock()
 317          self.saved = b5.clock, b5.pause, b4.cores_ok, main.read_frame
 318          b5.clock, b5.pause, b4.cores_ok = self.c, self.c.sleep, lambda: True
 319  
 320      def tearDown(self):
 321          b5.clock, b5.pause, b4.cores_ok, main.read_frame = self.saved
 322  
 323      def run_loop(self, plan, arm='B', work_s=120, status=(), persons=None, fail_start=False, battery=90, start_s=0.0,
 324                   read_s=0.0, hook=None, ready=None):
 325          t0 = self.c()
 326          heat = FakeHeat(self.c, [(t0 + a, x) for a, x in plan], [(t0 + a, s) for a, s in status])
 327          robot = FakeRobot(self.c, {t0 + a: b for a, b in (persons or {}).items()}, fail_start, start_s)
 328          main.read_frame = robot.read
 329          m = b5.Mission(arm, t0, work_s, ITEMS)
 330          robot.m = m
 331          rec, rows, oks = {'errors': [], 'camera_stops': [], 'readings': []}, [], []
 332          cam = {'on': True, 'slot': 1}
 333  
 334          def read(tag):
 335              rec['readings'].append({'tag': tag, 'camera': cam['on'], 't': m.rel(self.c()), 'ok': m.ok})
 336              self.c.sleep(read_s)  # a root reading takes time
 337              if hook:
 338                  hook(tag, m)
 339              if battery < b5.BATTERY_STOP_PCT:
 340                  rec['errors'].append('battery')
 341                  return False
 342              return True
 343          real_set_ok = m.set_ok
 344          m.set_ok = lambda ok: (oks.append((m.rel(self.c()), ok)), real_set_ok(ok))
 345          self.last_m = m
 346          b5.mission_loop(robot, heat, m, rec, rows.append, read, cam, ready or (lambda: None))
 347          return m, rec, rows, robot, heat, oks, cam
 348  
 349      def test_pause_start_and_resume(self):
 350          m, rec, rows, robot, heat, oks, cam = self.run_loop([(0, 'ACTIVE'), (20, 'PAUSE'), (60, 'ACTIVE')])
 351          self.assertEqual(rec['errors'], [])
 352          self.assertEqual([(r['stage'], r['from'], r['to']) for r in rows if r['kind'] == 'heat'],
 353                           [('intent', 'ACTIVE', 'PAUSE'), ('done', 'ACTIVE', 'PAUSE'), ('intent', 'PAUSE', 'ACTIVE'),
 354                            ('done', 'PAUSE', 'ACTIVE')])
 355          self.assertEqual((robot.stops, robot.starts, cam), (1, 1, {'on': True, 'slot': 2}))
 356          self.assertIs(robot.ok_at_stop, False)  # picker forbidden before the camera stops (main.py order)
 357          self.assertEqual([r['tag'] for r in rec['readings']], ['pause_start', 'pause_end'])
 358          self.assertEqual([r['camera'] for r in rec['readings']], [False, True])
 359          self.assertEqual(heat.events, ['camera_stop_ok', 'camera_start_ok'])
 360          cyc = [r['t'] for r in rows if r['kind'] == 'cycle']
 361          self.assertFalse([t for t in cyc if 20 < t < 60])  # no YOLO in the pause
 362          self.assertEqual({r['slot'] for r in rows if r['kind'] == 'cycle' and r['t'] > 60}, {2})
 363          paused = [t for t, ok in oks if not ok]
 364          self.assertTrue(paused and all(20 <= t < 62 for t in paused))
 365          self.assertTrue(m.ok)
 366          self.assertLess(self.c() - m.deadline, 2.0)  # the last move is cut at the deadline
 367          n, first, total = b5.pauses(rows, 120)
 368          self.assertTrue(n == 1 and 20 <= first < 22 and 38 < total < 42)
 369  
 370      def test_start_barrier_initially_active_serves_the_0_s_mark(self):
 371          got = []
 372  
 373          def ready():  # what the scheduler's first poll does once it is started here
 374              m = self.last_m
 375              got.append((m.ok, m.offer_due(self.c()), m.start(self.c(), 30.0)))
 376          m, *_ = self.run_loop([(0, 'ACTIVE')], arm='A', work_s=10, ready=ready)
 377          self.assertEqual(len(got), 1)
 378          self.assertTrue(got[0][0])
 379          self.assertEqual(got[0][2][0]['id'], 1)
 380          self.assertEqual(m.ticks['decided'], 1)
 381  
 382      def test_start_barrier_initially_forbidden_does_not(self):
 383          got = []
 384  
 385          def ready():
 386              m = self.last_m
 387              got.append((m.ok, m.offer_due(self.c()), m.start(self.c(), 30.0)))
 388          m, *_ = self.run_loop([(0, 'ACTIVE')], arm='A', work_s=10, status=[(0, 3)], ready=ready)
 389          self.assertEqual((got[0][0], got[0][2], m.ticks['paused']), (False, None, 1))
 390  
 391      def test_end_before_the_first_assessment_never_starts_the_scheduler(self):
 392          got = []
 393          self.run_loop([(0, 'END')], ready=lambda: got.append(1))
 394          self.assertEqual(got, [])
 395  
 396      def test_resume_allows_before_the_reading_a_mark_inside_it_is_served(self):
 397          seen = {}
 398  
 399          def hook(tag, m):  # the scheduler polling while the pause_end reading runs
 400              if tag == 'pause_end':
 401                  m.offer_due(self.c())
 402                  seen['go'] = m.start(self.c(), 30.0)
 403          m, rec, rows, robot, heat, oks, cam = self.run_loop([(0, 'ACTIVE'), (20, 'PAUSE'), (87, 'ACTIVE')], arm='A',
 404                                                              start_s=2.0, read_s=3.0, hook=hook)
 405          end = next(r for r in rec['readings'] if r['tag'] == 'pause_end')
 406          self.assertTrue(end['ok'])  # allowed before the reading
 407          self.assertTrue(87 < end['t'] < 90 < end['t'] + 3.0)  # the reading spans the 90 s mark
 408          self.assertIsNotNone(seen['go'])
 409          self.assertEqual(m.ticks['decided'], 1)
 410  
 411      def test_resume_with_b_event_pending(self):
 412          seen = {}
 413  
 414          def hook(tag, m):
 415              m.offer_due(self.c())
 416              seen[tag] = m.start(self.c(), 30.0)
 417          m, *_ = self.run_loop([(0, 'ACTIVE'), (20, 'PAUSE'), (60, 'ACTIVE')], start_s=2.0, read_s=3.0, hook=hook)
 418          self.assertIsNone(seen['pause_start'])  # pending, forbidden
 419          self.assertEqual(seen['pause_end'][0]['id'], 7)  # the newest pending event (offered at 60 s), at resume
 420  
 421      def test_reading_failure_at_resume_forbids_at_once(self):
 422          m, rec, *_ = self.run_loop([(0, 'ACTIVE'), (10, 'PAUSE')], battery=20)
 423          self.assertFalse(m.ok)
 424  
 425          def boom(tag, m):
 426              if tag == 'pause_end':
 427                  raise RuntimeError('root read')
 428          with self.assertRaises(RuntimeError):
 429              self.run_loop([(0, 'ACTIVE'), (10, 'PAUSE'), (50, 'ACTIVE')], hook=boom)
 430          self.assertFalse(self.last_m.ok)
 431  
 432      def test_pause_counts_restart_time_and_failed_restart_is_no_resume(self):
 433          m, rec, rows, *_ = self.run_loop([(0, 'ACTIVE'), (20, 'PAUSE'), (60, 'ACTIVE')], start_s=2.0, read_s=3.0)
 434          intent = [r for r in rows if r['kind'] == 'heat' and r['stage'] == 'intent']
 435          done = [r for r in rows if r['kind'] == 'heat' and r['stage'] == 'done' and r['to'] == 'ACTIVE']
 436          self.assertAlmostEqual(done[0]['t'] - intent[1]['t'], 2.0, places=3)  # the restart is pause time
 437          n, first, total = b5.pauses(rows, 120)
 438          self.assertAlmostEqual(total, done[0]['t'] - intent[0]['t'], places=3)
 439          with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as d:
 440              x = b5.metrics({**arm_record(), 'mission_end_s': 120}, rows, {}, Path(d))
 441          self.assertEqual((x['pauses'], x['resumes']), (1, 1))
 442          m, rec, rows, *_ = self.run_loop([(0, 'ACTIVE'), (10, 'PAUSE'), (50, 'ACTIVE')], fail_start=True, start_s=2.0)
 443          n, first, total = b5.pauses(rows, 52.0)
 444          self.assertEqual((n, round(total, 3)), (1, round(52.0 - first, 3)))  # open to the arm end
 445          with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as d:
 446              x = b5.metrics({**arm_record(), 'mission_end_s': 52.0}, rows, {}, Path(d))
 447          self.assertEqual(x['resumes'], 0)
 448  
 449      def test_transition_crossing_the_deadline_starts_no_cycle(self):
 450          m, rec, rows, robot, *_ = self.run_loop([(0, 'ACTIVE'), (100, 'PAUSE'), (114, 'ACTIVE')], work_s=118,
 451                                                  start_s=2.0, read_s=3.0)
 452          self.assertEqual((rec['errors'], robot.starts), ([], 1))
 453          self.assertTrue([r for r in rec['readings'] if r['tag'] == 'pause_end'])  # the transition finished
 454          self.assertFalse([r for r in rows if r['kind'] == 'cycle' and r['t'] >= 100])
 455  
 456      def test_detection_ending_after_the_deadline_offers_no_event(self):
 457          m, rec, rows, *_ = self.run_loop([(0, 'ACTIVE')], work_s=5.2, persons={5.0: 'far'})
 458          self.assertEqual(max(r['t'] for r in rows if r['kind'] == 'cycle'), 5.1)
 459          self.assertEqual((rec['late_not_offered'], [e for e in m.events if e['source'] == 'real']), (1, []))
 460          with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as d:
 461              x = b5.metrics({**arm_record(), 'late_not_offered': 1}, rows, {}, Path(d))
 462          self.assertEqual(x['events']['real']['late_not_offered'], 1)

 464      def test_pause_open_at_the_end_counts_to_the_end(self):
 465          rows = [hrow('intent', 'ACTIVE', 'PAUSE', 100.0), hrow('done', 'ACTIVE', 'PAUSE', 100.4),
 466                  hrow('intent', 'PAUSE', 'HOLD', 120.0), hrow('done', 'PAUSE', 'HOLD', 120.0),
 467                  hrow('intent', 'HOLD', 'ACTIVE', 150.0),
 468                  hrow('done', 'HOLD', 'ACTIVE', 152.0), hrow('intent', 'ACTIVE', 'PAUSE', 1700.0),
 469                  hrow('done', 'ACTIVE', 'PAUSE', 1700.4)]
 470          self.assertEqual(b5.pauses(rows, 1800.0), (2, 100.0, 152.0))  # 100 -> 152 (restart done), 1700 -> end
 471

 495      def test_server_repinned_at_every_camera_start(self):
 496          calls = []
 497          t0 = self.c()
 498          heat = FakeHeat(self.c, [(t0, 'ACTIVE'), (t0 + 10, 'PAUSE'), (t0 + 50, 'ACTIVE'), (t0 + 70, 'PAUSE'),
 499                                   (t0 + 110, 'ACTIVE')])
 500          robot = FakeRobot(self.c)
 501          main.read_frame = robot.read
 502          m = b5.Mission('B', t0, 120, ITEMS)
 503          rec = {'errors': [], 'camera_stops': [], 'readings': []}
 504          b5.mission_loop(robot, heat, m, rec, lambda r: None, lambda t: True, {'on': True, 'slot': 1},
 505                          repin=lambda: calls.append(robot.capturing))
 506          self.assertEqual(calls, [True, True])  # after each successful camera start
 507          robot = FakeRobot(self.c, fail_start=True)
 508          main.read_frame = robot.read
 509          calls.clear()
 510          t0 = self.c()
 511          heat = FakeHeat(self.c, [(t0, 'ACTIVE'), (t0 + 10, 'PAUSE'), (t0 + 50, 'ACTIVE')])
 512          b5.mission_loop(robot, heat, b5.Mission('B', t0, 120, ITEMS), {'errors': [], 'camera_stops': [],
 513                          'readings': []}, lambda r: None, lambda t: True, {'on': True, 'slot': 1},
 514                          repin=lambda: calls.append(1))
 515          self.assertEqual(calls, [])  # a failed start is not followed by a re-pin
 516  
 517      def test_hold_and_pause_hold(self):

 519          self.assertEqual([(r['from'], r['to']) for r in rows if r['kind'] == 'heat' and r['stage'] == 'intent'],
 520                           [('ACTIVE', 'HOLD'), ('HOLD', 'PAUSE'), ('PAUSE', 'ACTIVE')])

 527                                   'The person left your view.'])

 531      def test_no_new_frames_limit(self):
 532          t0 = self.c()
 533          heat = FakeHeat(self.c, [(t0, 'ACTIVE')])
 534          robot = FakeRobot(self.c)
 535          main.read_frame = lambda **kw: {'status': 'ok', 'session': 's', 'frame': 7, 'age_s': 0.1, 'image': Image()}
 536          m = b5.Mission('A', t0, 60, ITEMS)
 537          rec, rows = {'errors': [], 'camera_stops': [], 'readings': []}, []
 538          b5.mission_loop(robot, heat, m, rec, rows.append, lambda t: True, {'on': True, 'slot': 1})
 539          self.assertEqual([r['reason'] for r in rows if r['kind'] == 'camera_stop'], ['no new'] * 4)
 540          self.assertIn('camera stop: no new', rec['errors'][0])

 545  def arm_record(**kw):
 546      good = test_b4.good
 547      rec = {'arm': 'A', 'errors': [], 'cores_before': True, 'cores_after': True, 'agents_before': '',
 548             'agents_after': '', 'overrun_s': 3.0, 'watch': test_b4.watch(), 'work_s': 1800, 'mission_end_s': 1800,
 549             'readings': [{**good(mem=('runner', 'llama_server', 'robotcam')), 'tag': 'start', 'camera': True, 't': 0},
 550                          {**good(mem=('runner', 'llama_server')), 'tag': 'pause_start', 'camera': False, 't': 400},
 551                          {**good(mem=('runner', 'llama_server', 'robotcam')), 'tag': 'pause_end', 'camera': True,
 552                           't': 500},
 553                          {**good(mem=('runner', 'llama_server', 'robotcam'), mono=1800), 'tag': 'end',
 554                           'camera': True, 't': 1800}]}
 555      return {**rec, **kw}

 561  def hrow(stage, frm, to, t):
 562      return {'kind': 'heat', 'stage': stage, 'from': frm, 'to': to, 't': t}

 565  class Repin(unittest.TestCase):
 566      """Owner decision 2026-11-15: re-pin like a robot; drift corrected by one re-pin is VALID and recorded."""
 567  
 568      def setUp(self):
 569          self.d = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
 570          self.proc = Path(self.d.name)
 571          for tid in (11, 12, 13):
 572              (self.proc / '4242' / 'task' / str(tid)).mkdir(parents=True)
 573          self.aff = {11: {0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7}, 12: {4, 5, 6, 7}, 13: {0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7}}
 574          self.patches = [unittest.mock.patch.object(b4, 'PROC', self.proc),
 575                          unittest.mock.patch.object(b5.os, 'sched_getaffinity', self.get),
 576                          unittest.mock.patch.object(b5.os, 'sched_setaffinity', self.set)]
 577          for p in self.patches:
 578              p.start()
 579          self.fail = set()
 580  
 581      def tearDown(self):
 582          for p in self.patches:
 583              p.stop()
 584          self.d.cleanup()
 585  
 586      def get(self, tid):
 587          if tid not in self.aff:
 588              raise ProcessLookupError(tid)
 589          return set(self.aff[tid])
 590  
 591      def set(self, tid, cpus):
 592          if tid in self.fail:
 593              raise PermissionError('EPERM')
 594          self.aff[tid] = set(cpus)
 595  
 596      def test_server_repin_records_before_after(self):
 597          rp = b5.Repinner({'server_pid': 4242})
 598          e = rp.server('watch')
 599          self.assertTrue(e['ok'])
 600          self.assertEqual((e['threads'], e['before'][11], e['after'][11]), ([11, 13], [0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7],
 601                                                                             [4, 5, 6, 7]))
 602          self.assertEqual({t: sorted(c) for t, c in self.aff.items()}, {t: [4, 5, 6, 7] for t in (11, 12, 13)})
 603          self.assertEqual(rp.events, [e])
 604  
 605      def test_server_repin_failure_and_vanished_thread(self):
 606          self.fail.add(13)
 607          del self.aff[12]  # ended after the listing: skipped
 608          e = b5.Repinner({'server_pid': 4242}).server('arm_start')
 609          self.assertFalse(e['ok'])
 610          self.assertIn(13, e['errors'])
 611          self.assertNotIn(12, e['before'])
 612  
 613      def test_detector_repin_uses_check_affinity(self):
 614          det = unittest.mock.Mock()
 615          det.check_affinity.return_value = {'before': {1: [0, 1]}, 'repinned': [1], 'after': {1: [4, 5]}, 'ok': True,
 616                                             'errors': {}}
 617          e = b5.Repinner({'detector': det}).detector('watch')
 618          self.assertEqual((e['process'], e['threads'], e['ok']), ('detector', [1], True))
 619  
 620      def test_camera_start_detector_repin_is_recorded(self):
 621          seen = []
 622          heat = unittest.mock.Mock()
 623          heat.event = lambda name, detail=None, dump=None: seen.append(name)
 624          rp = b5.Repinner({})
 625          b5.record_camera_repins(heat, rp)
 626          heat.event('detector_affinity', {'before': {}, 'repinned': [7], 'after': {}, 'ok': False, 'errors': {7: 'x'}})
 627          heat.event('camera_start_ok')
 628          self.assertEqual(seen, ['detector_affinity', 'camera_start_ok'])
 629          self.assertEqual([(e['trigger'], e['ok']) for e in rp.events], [('camera_start', False)])

 631      def test_groups_recorded(self):
 632          (self.proc / '4242' / 'cpuset').write_text('/top-app\n')
 633          (self.proc / '4242' / 'cgroup').write_text('0::/uid_1/pid_4242\n')
 634          g = b5.groups({'server_pid': 4242})
 635          self.assertEqual((g['llama_server_cpuset'], g['llama_server_cgroup']), ('/top-app', '0::/uid_1/pid_4242'))
 636          self.assertIn('Error', g['runner_cpuset'])  # not in the fake tree: error text, not a crash
 637  
 638      def test_scan_splits_drift_and_repins_it(self):
 639          hits = ['agent pid 9: claude', 'llama-server thread 11 Cpus_allowed_list:\t0-7',
 640                  'llama-server thread 13 affinity unreadable: x', "detector threads {5: [0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7]}"]
 641          rp = unittest.mock.Mock()
 642          rp.server.return_value, rp.detector.return_value = {'p': 's'}, {'p': 'd'}
 643          with unittest.mock.patch.object(b4, 'watch_check', lambda t: {'mono': 1.0, 'hits': list(hits), 'vanished': 0}):
 644              x = b5.scan({'server_pid': 4242}, rp)
 645          self.assertEqual(x['hits'], [hits[0], hits[2]])  # not drift: still hits
 646          self.assertEqual(sorted(x['drift']), ['detector', 'llama_server'])
 647          self.assertEqual(x['repins'], [{'p': 'd'}, {'p': 's'}])
 648          self.assertIn('llama_server_cpuset', x['groups'])
 649  
 650      def watch(self, drifts):
 651          return {**test_b4.watch(), 'samples': [{'mono': 30.0 * i, 'hits': [], 'drift': {p: ['x'] for p in d}}
 652                                                 for i, d in enumerate(drifts)]}
 653  
 654      def test_summary(self):
 655          rec = arm_record(watch={**self.watch([['llama_server'], []]), 'samples': [
 656              {'mono': 0, 'hits': [], 'drift': {'llama_server': ['x']}, 'groups': {'llama_server_cpuset': '/top-app'}},
 657              {'mono': 30, 'hits': [], 'drift': {}, 'groups': {'llama_server_cpuset': '/foreground'}}]},
 658              repins=[{'trigger': 'watch', 'process': 'llama_server', 'threads': [11], 'ok': True},
 659                      {'trigger': 'arm_start', 'process': 'llama_server', 'threads': [], 'ok': True}])
 660          x = b5.repin_summary(rec)
 661          self.assertEqual(x, {'acted': {'watch/llama_server': 1}, 'failed': 0, 'drifted_scans': {'llama_server': 1},
 662                               'groups_seen': {'llama_server_cpuset': ['/foreground', '/top-app']}})
 663  
 664      def test_validity(self):
 665          ok = {'trigger': 'watch', 'process': 'llama_server', 'ok': True}
 666          bad = {**ok, 'ok': False, 'errors': {13: 'EPERM'}}
 667          # drift corrected by one re-pin: VALID, the event recorded
 668          rec = arm_record(watch=self.watch([[], ['llama_server'], [], ['detector'], []]), repins=[ok, ok])
 669          self.assertEqual(b5.arm_problems(rec, [CYC]), [])
 670          # the same process drifted in two consecutive scans: INVALID
 671          rec = arm_record(watch=self.watch([[], ['llama_server'], ['llama_server'], []]), repins=[ok, ok])
 672          self.assertTrue(any('two consecutive scans' in p for p in b5.arm_problems(rec, [CYC])))
 673          # different processes in consecutive scans: each corrected once, VALID
 674          rec = arm_record(watch=self.watch([['llama_server'], ['detector']]), repins=[ok, ok])
 675          self.assertEqual(b5.arm_problems(rec, [CYC]), [])
 676          # a failed re-pin (watch, arm start or camera start): INVALID
 677          for trig in ('watch', 'arm_start', 'camera_start'):
 678              rec = arm_record(watch=self.watch([[]]), repins=[{**bad, 'trigger': trig}])
 679              self.assertTrue(any('re-pin failed' in p for p in b5.arm_problems(rec, [CYC])), trig)
 680          rec = arm_record(repins=[bad])
 681          del rec['watch']
 682          self.assertTrue(any('re-pin failed' in p for p in b5.arm_problems(rec, [CYC])))
 683

 685  class Labels(unittest.TestCase):
 686      def test_valid(self):
 687          self.assertEqual(b5.arm_problems(arm_record(), [CYC]), [])
 688  
 689      def test_invalid(self):
 690          bad = [arm_record(errors=['heat END: x']), arm_record(agents_after='1 claude'), arm_record(cores_before=False),
 691                 arm_record(overrun_s=15.5), arm_record(watch=test_b4.watch(['agent pid 3'])),
 692                 arm_record(readings=arm_record()['readings'][1:]), arm_record(camera_end={'error': 'not stopped'})]
 693          for rec in bad:
 694              self.assertTrue(b5.arm_problems(rec, [CYC]), rec)
 695          self.assertTrue(b5.arm_problems(arm_record(), []))  # no camera cycles
 696          self.assertTrue(b5.arm_problems(arm_record(), [CYC, {**CYC, 'cores_ok': False}]))
 697          self.assertTrue(b5.arm_problems(arm_record(), [CYC, {'kind': 'decision', 'error': 'x'}]))
 698          self.assertTrue(b5.arm_problems(arm_record(), [CYC, {'kind': 'camera_stop', 'reason': 'stale'}]))
 699          self.assertEqual(b5.arm_problems(arm_record(), [CYC] + [{'kind': 'camera_stop', 'reason': 'no new'}] * 3), [])
 700          self.assertTrue(b5.arm_problems(arm_record(), [CYC] + [{'kind': 'camera_stop', 'reason': 'no new'}] * 4))

 702      def test_robotcam_memory_needed_while_the_camera_is_on(self):
 703          r = arm_record()
 704          r['readings'][2] = {**test_b4.good(mem=('runner', 'llama_server')), 'tag': 'pause_end', 'camera': True}
 705          self.assertTrue(any('robotcam' in p for p in b5.arm_problems(r, [CYC])))
 706          r['readings'][1]['mem_runner'] = {'Pss': 1, 'pids': [1], 'rc': 0, 'missing': ['pid 1 VmHWM']}
 707          self.assertTrue(any('pause_start' in p for p in b5.arm_problems(r, [CYC])))

 710  class Metrics(unittest.TestCase):
 711      def test_counts_latency_agreement(self):
 712          ev = [{'kind': 'event', 'source': 'scripted', 'fate': 'served', 'latency_s': 4.0, 'wait_s': 0.1},
 713                {'kind': 'event', 'source': 'scripted', 'fate': 'dropped'},
 714                {'kind': 'event', 'source': 'real', 'fate': 'served', 'latency_s': 8.0, 'wait_s': 4.0},
 715                {'kind': 'event', 'source': 'real', 'fate': 'unserved'}]
 716          dec = [{'kind': 'decision', 'source': 'scripted', 'id': 2, 'top': 'explore', 'wall_s': 3.9},
 717                 {'kind': 'decision', 'source': 'real', 'id': 'R1', 'top': 'approach', 'wall_s': 4.2},
 718                 {'kind': 'decision', 'source': 'scripted', 'id': 3, 'cancelled': True, 'wall_s': 1.0}]
 719          cyc = [{**CYC, 't': 0.0, 'size': 640, 'detect_ms': 500}, {**CYC, 't': 1.7, 'size': 320, 'detect_ms': 140},
 720                 {**CYC, 't': 900.0, 'slot': 2, 'size': 640, 'detect_ms': 520},
 721                 {**CYC, 't': 901.8, 'slot': 2, 'size': 320, 'detect_ms': 150},
 722                 hrow('intent', 'ACTIVE', 'PAUSE', 600.0), hrow('done', 'PAUSE', 'ACTIVE', 899.0)]
 723          with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as d:
 724              x = b5.metrics(arm_record(), ev + dec + cyc, {2: 'wait'}, Path(d))
 725          self.assertEqual(x['decisions_served'], 2)
 726          self.assertEqual(x['events']['scripted'], {'served': 1, 'dropped': 1, 'cancelled': 0, 'unserved': 0, 'error': 0,
 727                                                     'offered': 2})
 728          self.assertEqual(x['latency_s']['all']['n'], 2)
 729          self.assertEqual(x['agreement'], {'compared': 1, 'agree': 0, 'ids_differ': [2]})
 730          self.assertEqual((x['pauses'], x['first_pause_s'], x['pause_s'], x['active_share']), (1, 600.0, 299.0, 0.834))
 731          self.assertEqual(x['cycle_period_s']['n'], 2)
 732          self.assertEqual(x['detect_ms'][640]['n'], 2)
 733          self.assertEqual(x['mem_available_min_kb'], None)
 734

 739                                                             {'event': 'dumpsys', 'skin_c': 37.4},
 740                                                             {'event': 'start', 'skin_c': 40.0}]))
 741              self.assertEqual(b5.trace_max(p), 37.4)

 750  class Sessions(unittest.TestCase):
 751      def setUp(self):
 752          self.saved = b5.gate, b5.run_arm, b4.reading, b4.cores_ok, b5.repro.agents, b5.cr.wait_cores
 753          self.ran = []
 754          b4.reading = lambda **kw: test_b4.good()
 755          b4.cores_ok = lambda: True
 756          b5.repro.agents = lambda: ''
 757          b5.cr.wait_cores = lambda label: None
 758          b5.gate = lambda ref, label: {'passed': True}
 759  
 760      def tearDown(self):
 761          b5.gate, b5.run_arm, b4.reading, b4.cores_ok, b5.repro.agents, b5.cr.wait_cores = self.saved
 762  
 763      def fake_arms(self, labels, battery_stop=False):
 764          def run_arm(k, out, ctx, emit):
 765              self.ran.append(k)
 766              return {'arm': k, 'label': labels.get(k, 'VALID'), 'problems': [], 'battery_stop': battery_stop}, []
 767          b5.run_arm = run_arm
 768  
 769      def session(self, out, smoke=False):
 770          with open(out / 'arms.jsonl', 'a') as alog, unittest.mock.patch('sys.stdout', io.StringIO()):
 771              return b5.session_loop(out, {'start': {'skin': 30.0}}, {'smoke': smoke, 'session': 1}, lambda r: None, alog)
 772  
 773      def test_order_invalid_stops_and_resume_redoes_it(self):
 774          with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as d:
 775              out = Path(d)
 776              self.fake_arms({'A': 'INVALID'})
 777              self.assertIn('arm A INVALID', self.session(out))
 778              self.assertEqual(self.ran, ['A'])
 779              self.fake_arms({})
 780              self.assertEqual(self.session(out), 'all arms done')
 781              self.assertEqual(self.ran, ['A', 'A', 'B'])
 782  
 783      def test_smoke_gates_only_the_first_arm(self):
 784          with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as d:
 785              self.fake_arms({k: b5.SMOKE_LABEL for k in b5.ARMS})
 786              gated = []
 787              b5.gate = lambda ref, label: gated.append(label) or {'passed': True}
 788              b5.repro.agents = lambda: '42 claude'
 789              self.assertEqual(self.session(Path(d), smoke=True), 'all arms done')
 790              self.assertEqual((self.ran, gated), (['A', 'B'], ['arm A']))
 791  
 792      def test_timed_gates_every_arm_and_safety_before_the_arm(self):
 793          with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as d:
 794              self.fake_arms({})
 795              gated = []
 796              b5.gate = lambda ref, label: gated.append(label) or {'passed': True}
 797              self.assertEqual(self.session(Path(d)), 'all arms done')
 798              self.assertEqual(gated, ['arm A', 'arm B'])
 799          with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as d:
 800              self.ran.clear()
 801              b4.reading = lambda **kw: test_b4.good(battery=29)
 802              self.assertIn('stopped before arm A', self.session(Path(d)))
 803              b4.reading = lambda **kw: test_b4.good()
 804              b5.repro.agents = lambda: '42 claude'
 805              self.assertIn('agent processes running', self.session(Path(d)))
 806              self.assertEqual(self.ran, [])
 807  
 808      def test_battery_stop_in_an_arm_ends_the_session(self):
 809          with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as d:
 810              self.fake_arms({'A': 'INVALID'}, battery_stop=True)
 811              self.assertIn('battery below 30 %', self.session(Path(d)))
 812              self.assertEqual(self.ran, ['A'])

 814      def test_definition_and_resume_refusal(self):
 815          with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as d:
 816              old = b5.definition(Path(d))
 817              self.assertIn('benchmark/mind_picker/b5_mission/oneshot.sh', old['files'])
 818              self.assertIn('benchmark/mind_picker/b2_rules/runs/20261102T143251Z/requests.jsonl', old['files'])
 819              self.assertIn('heat_control.py', old['files'])
 820              self.assertIn('--slot-save-path', old['commands']['server'])
 821              new = json.loads(json.dumps(old))
 822              new['files']['heat_control.py'] = '0' * 64
 823              self.assertEqual(len(b4.definition_diff(old, new)), 1)
 824  
 825      def test_battery_first_start_85_resume_50_smoke_none(self):
 826          self.assertIsNone(b5.battery_refusal(85, False, None))
 827          self.assertIn('< 85 %', b5.battery_refusal(84, False, None))
 828          self.assertIsNone(b5.battery_refusal(50, False, '20261110T000000Z'))
 829          self.assertIn('< 50 %', b5.battery_refusal(49, False, '20261110T000000Z'))
 830          self.assertIsNone(b5.battery_refusal(40, True, None))
 831  
 832      def test_smoke_thresholds_lower_the_deployed_constants_at_runtime(self):
 833          saved = hc.SKIN_PAUSE_C, hc.SKIN_RESUME_C
 834          try:
 835              t = b5.smoke_thresholds(31.24)  # the arm-start skin
 836              self.assertEqual((t['pause_c'], t['resume_c'], t['min_active_s'], t['min_pause_s']), (32.24, 31.74, 60, 30))
 837              st = {'phase': 'ACTIVE', 'since': 0.0}
 838              now = 100.0
 839              rd = {'dump': {'t': now, 'status': 0, 'skin': 32.3}, 'fast': [{'t': now, 'cpu_c': 50.0, 'bat_c': 30.0}]}
 840              self.assertEqual(hc.decide(now, rd, st)[0], 'PAUSE')
 841              self.assertEqual(hc.decide(59.0, {**rd, 'dump': {**rd['dump'], 't': 59.0},
 842                                                'fast': [{'t': 59.0, 'cpu_c': 50.0, 'bat_c': 30.0}]}, st)[0], 'ACTIVE')
 843              rd['dump']['skin'] = 31.8
 844              self.assertEqual(hc.decide(now, rd, {'phase': 'PAUSE', 'since': 60.0})[0], 'PAUSE')
 845              rd['dump']['skin'] = 31.74
 846              self.assertEqual(hc.decide(now, rd, {'phase': 'PAUSE', 'since': 60.0})[0], 'ACTIVE')
 847          finally:
 848              hc.SKIN_PAUSE_C, hc.SKIN_RESUME_C = saved
 849          self.assertEqual((hc.SKIN_PAUSE_C, hc.SKIN_RESUME_C), (37.0, 35.0))
 850  
 851      def test_smoke_report_says_whether_a_pause_happened(self):
 852          run = {'utc': 'U', 'smoke': True, 'work_s': 180, 'git_head': 'h', 'start': test_b4.good(),
 853                 'definition': {'files': {}, 'commands': {'server': 'srv'}}}
 854          rec = {**arm_record(label=b5.SMOKE_LABEL, session=1, problems=[], heat_thresholds={'pause_c': 31.0}),
 855                 'metrics': {}}
 856          with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as d:
 857              rec['metrics'] = b5.metrics(rec, [{**CYC, 't': 0.0, 'size': 320, 'detect_ms': 140}], {}, Path(d))
 858          text = b5.report(run, [rec])
 859          self.assertIn('SMOKE HEAT THRESHOLDS', text)
 860          self.assertIn('NO PAUSE HAPPENED', text)
 861          self.assertIn("{'pause_c': 31.0}", text)
 862          rows = [{**CYC, 't': 0.0, 'size': 320, 'detect_ms': 140},
 863                  hrow('intent', 'ACTIVE', 'PAUSE', 61.0)]
 864          with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as d:
 865              rec['metrics'] = b5.metrics(rec, rows, {}, Path(d))
 866          text = b5.report(run, [rec])
 867          self.assertIn('1 pause(s) with camera stop, 0 resume(s)', text)
 868          self.assertIn('NO RESUME HAPPENED', text)
 869          rows.append(hrow('intent', 'PAUSE', 'ACTIVE', 93.0))  # an attempt is no resume
 870          with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as d:
 871              rec['metrics'] = b5.metrics(rec, rows, {}, Path(d))
 872          self.assertIn('NO RESUME HAPPENED', b5.report(run, [rec]))
 873          rows.append(hrow('done', 'PAUSE', 'ACTIVE', 95.0))
 874          with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as d:
 875              rec['metrics'] = b5.metrics(rec, rows, {}, Path(d))
 876          text = b5.report(run, [rec])
 877          self.assertIn('1 pause(s) with camera stop, 1 resume(s)', text)
 878          self.assertNotIn('HAPPENED', text)
 879  
 880      def test_early_refusal_record_saves_and_reports(self):
 881          with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as d, unittest.mock.patch.object(b4, 'cores_ok', lambda: False), \
 882                  unittest.mock.patch.object(b5.repro, 'agents', lambda: ''):
 883              out = Path(d)

 885                                     lambda r: None)
 886              with open(out / 'arms.jsonl', 'a') as f:
 887                  f.write(json.dumps(rec) + '\n')
 888              run = {'utc': 'U', 'smoke': False, 'work_s': 1800, 'git_head': 'h', 'start': test_b4.good(),
 889                     'definition': {'files': {}, 'commands': {'server': 'srv'}}}
 890              text = b5.report(run, b4.read_jsonl(out / 'arms.jsonl'))
 891          self.assertEqual((rec['label'], rows), ('INVALID', []))
 892          self.assertEqual(set(rec['metrics']), set(b5.metrics(arm_record(), [], {}, Path('.'))))
 893          self.assertIn('== A: INVALID', text)
 894          self.assertIn('initial agent/cores check failed', text)

 901  # ---------------------------------------------------------------- one arm, real time, everything faked
 902  
 903  class FakeMonitor:
 904      """heat_control.HeatMonitor's interface; skin follows a plan by seconds since start()."""
 905      plan = [(0, 30.0)]
 906  
 907      def __init__(self):
 908          self.on_dump, self.t0, self.stopped = None, None, False
 909  
 910      def start(self):
 911          self.t0 = time.monotonic()
 912  
 913      def stop(self):
 914          self.stopped = True
 915  
 916      status = [(0, 0)]
 917  
 918      def readings(self):
 919          now = time.monotonic()
 920          skin = [s for at, s in self.plan if now - self.t0 >= at][-1]
 921          status = [s for at, s in self.status if now - self.t0 >= at][-1]
 922          return {'dump': {'t': now, 'status': status, 'skin': skin}, 'fast': [{'t': now, 'cpu_c': 50.0, 'bat_c': 30.0}]}
 923  
 924  
 925  class Watcher:
 926      def __init__(self, targets):
 927          self.targets = targets
 928  
 929      def start(self):
 930          return self
 931  
 932      def stop(self):
 933          return test_b4.watch()
 934  
 935  
 936  class RealTimeArm(unittest.TestCase):
 937      def setUp(self):
 938          self.patches = [unittest.mock.patch.object(*p) for p in [
 939              (b5, 'OFFER_S', 0.2), (b5, 'TICK_S', 0.5), (b5, 'POLL_S', 0.01), (main, 'CYCLE_MOVE_TIME', 0.05),
 940              (hc, 'MIN_ACTIVE_S', 0.6), (hc, 'MIN_PAUSE_S', 0.4), (hc, 'HeatMonitor', FakeMonitor),
 941              (hc, 'SKIN_PAUSE_C', hc.SKIN_PAUSE_C), (hc, 'SKIN_RESUME_C', hc.SKIN_RESUME_C),  # restored after
 942              (main, 'get_detector', lambda pinned: None), (b4, 'server_command', lambda out, k: ['srv']),
 943              (b4, 'start_server', lambda out, name, cmd: (unittest.mock.Mock(pid=4242), None, 0.1)),
 944              (b4, 'stop_server', lambda proc, log: (0, False)), (b4, 'warm', lambda prefix: {'save': {}}),
 945              (b5, 'picker_setup', lambda test: ({('V3', 'FWD', it['id']): ('t', [1], {}) for it in ITEMS}, [1])),
 946              (b4, 'reading', lambda pids=None, light=False, tag='': {**test_b4.good(
 947                  mem=('runner', 'llama_server', 'robotcam')), 'mono': time.monotonic()}),
 948              (b4, 'Periodic', lambda fn, period: Watcher(None)), (b4, 'cores_ok', lambda: True),
 949              (b5.Repinner, 'server', lambda self, trigger: self.add({'trigger': trigger, 'process': 'llama_server',
 950                                                                      'ok': True})), (b5.repro, 'agents', lambda: ''),
 951              (b4, 'slot', lambda action: {'action': action, 'wall_s': 0.0}),
 952              (b5.b2, 'score', self.score), (b5.b2, 'preflight', lambda post, s, blocks, info, tag: {
 953                  ('V3', 'FWD', it['id']): ('t', [1], {}) for it in s['items']}),
 954              (main, 'Robot', lambda motors: self.robot)]]
 955          for p in self.patches:
 956              p.start()
 957          self.robot = FakeRobot(test_b4.FakeClock(), {})
 958          self.robot.clock.sleep = lambda s: time.sleep(0.01)
 959          self.saved_read = main.read_frame
 960          main.read_frame = self.robot.read
 961          self.decisions = []
 962  
 963      def tearDown(self):
 964          for p in self.patches:
 965              p.stop()
 966          main.read_frame = self.saved_read
 967  
 968      def score(self, post, it, v, order, prompt):
 969          self.decisions.append((time.monotonic(), it['id']))
 970          for _ in range(2):  # V3: reason, then the action
 971              time.sleep(0.045)
 972              post('/completion', {})
 973          return {'top': 'explore', 'reason': 'r', 'reason_tokens': 3}
 974  
 975      def run_arm(self, arm, skin_plan, smoke=False, status=((0, 0),)):
 976          FakeMonitor.plan, FakeMonitor.status = skin_plan, list(status)
 977          with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as d, unittest.mock.patch('sys.stdout', io.StringIO()), \
 978                  unittest.mock.patch.object(b4, 'post', lambda path, body: {'timings': {}}):
 979              ctx = {'session': 1, 'smoke': smoke, 'dur': 4.0, 'test': {'missions': {'M1': 'm'}, 'items': ITEMS},
 980                     'record': {i: 'explore' for i in range(1, 41)}}
 981              rows = []
 982              rec, rows = b5.run_arm(arm, Path(d), ctx, rows.append)
 983              trace = (Path(d) / rec['heat_trace']).read_text()
 984          return rec, rows, trace
 985  
 986      def check_common(self, rec, rows, label='VALID'):
 987          self.assertEqual(rec['problems'], [])
 988          self.assertEqual(rec['label'], label)
 989          x = rec['metrics']
 990          self.assertEqual(x['events']['scripted']['offered'], 20)
 991          for s, f in x['events'].items():
 992              self.assertEqual(f['offered'], sum(v for k, v in f.items() if k not in ('offered', 'late_not_offered')))
 993          self.assertLessEqual(rec['readings'][0]['t'], 0)  # the start reading's real relative time (before t0)
 994          self.assertEqual(x['decisions_served'], sum(f['served'] for f in x['events'].values()))
 995          self.assertGreater(x['decisions_served'], 0)
 996          self.assertEqual(x['agreement']['ids_differ'], [])
 997          self.assertLessEqual(rec['overrun_s'], 1.0)
 998          self.assertEqual([r['tag'] for r in rec['readings']][0], 'start')
 999          self.assertEqual([r['tag'] for r in rec['readings']][-1], 'end')
1000          run = {'utc': 'U', 'smoke': False, 'work_s': 4, 'git_head': 'h', 'start': test_b4.good(),
1001                 'definition': {'files': {}, 'commands': {'server': 'srv'}}}
1002          text = b5.report(run, json.loads(json.dumps([rec])))  # as read back from arms.jsonl
1003          self.assertIn(f'== {rec["arm"]}: {label}', text)
1004          self.assertIn('agreement with B2 run of record', text)
1005  
1006      def test_arm_a_with_a_heat_pause(self):
1007          rec, rows, trace = self.run_arm('A', [(0, 30.0), (0.7, 38.0), (1.5, 34.0)])
1008          self.check_common(rec, rows)
1009          x = rec['metrics']
1010          self.assertEqual(x['pauses'], 1)
1011          self.assertEqual([r['tag'] for r in rec['readings']], ['start', 'pause_start', 'pause_end', 'end'])
1012          t_off = next(r['t'] for r in rows if r['kind'] == 'heat' and r['stage'] == 'intent')
1013          t_on = next(r['t'] for r in rows if r['kind'] == 'heat' and r['stage'] == 'done' and r['to'] == 'ACTIVE')
1014          dec = [r for r in rows if r['kind'] == 'decision']
1015          self.assertFalse([r for r in dec if t_off + 0.05 < r['started'] < t_on], 'a decision started in the pause')
1016          self.assertLessEqual(len(dec), 9)  # one per 0.5 s mark at most
1017          self.assertIn('"camera_stop_ok"', trace)
1018          self.assertEqual((self.robot.stops, self.robot.starts), (2, 2))  # pause + end; arm start + resume
1019          self.assertEqual([(e['trigger'], e['process']) for e in rec['repins']],
1020                           [('arm_start', 'llama_server'), ('camera_start', 'llama_server')])
1021  
1022      def test_smoke_thresholds_from_the_arm_start_skin_pause_and_resume(self):
1023          # the fake start reading's skin is 30.0: pause at 31.0, resume at 30.5; the deployed 37/35 never trigger here
1024          rec, rows, trace = self.run_arm('B', [(0, 30.0), (0.7, 31.2), (1.5, 30.4)], smoke=True)
1025          self.check_common(rec, rows, b5.SMOKE_LABEL)
1026          self.assertEqual((rec['heat_thresholds']['pause_c'], rec['heat_thresholds']['resume_c']), (31.0, 30.5))
1027          self.assertEqual((rec['metrics']['pauses'], rec['metrics']['resumes']), (1, 1))
1028          self.assertEqual((self.robot.stops, self.robot.starts), (2, 2))
1029          self.assertIn('"camera_start_ok"', trace)
1030  
1031      def test_timed_arm_keeps_the_deployed_thresholds(self):
1032          rec, rows, trace = self.run_arm('B', [(0, 30.0), (0.7, 31.2), (1.5, 30.4)])
1033          self.assertNotIn('heat_thresholds', rec)
1034          self.assertEqual(rec['metrics']['pauses'], 0)
1035          self.assertEqual((hc.SKIN_PAUSE_C, hc.SKIN_RESUME_C), (37.0, 35.0))
1036  
1037      def test_arm_a_serves_every_eligible_mark(self):
1038          rec, rows, trace = self.run_arm('A', [(0, 30.0)])
1039          self.check_common(rec, rows)
1040          self.assertEqual(rec['metrics']['ticks'], {'decided': 8, 'empty': 0, 'paused': 0, 'busy': 0})  # 0 .. 3.5 s
1041          first = min((r for r in rows if r['kind'] == 'decision'), key=lambda r: r['started'])
1042          self.assertEqual(first['id'], 1)
1043          self.assertLess(first['started'], 0.1)
1044  
1045      def test_arm_a_initially_severe_skips_the_forbidden_marks(self):
1046          rec, rows, trace = self.run_arm('A', [(0, 30.0)], status=[(0, 3), (0.75, 0)])
1047          self.check_common(rec, rows)
1048          self.assertEqual(rec['metrics']['ticks'], {'decided': 6, 'empty': 0, 'paused': 2, 'busy': 0})
1049          self.assertFalse([r for r in rows if r['kind'] == 'decision' and r['started'] < 0.7])
1050  
1051      def test_arm_b_skin_above_36_holds_events(self):
1052          rec, rows, trace = self.run_arm('B', [(0, 30.0), (2.0, 36.5)])
1053          self.check_common(rec, rows)
1054          dec = [r for r in rows if r['kind'] == 'decision' and r['fate'] == 'served']
1055          self.assertFalse([r for r in dec if r['started'] > 2.1], 'a decision started at skin >= 36')
1056          self.assertEqual(rec['metrics']['pauses'], 0)
1057          self.assertEqual(rec['metrics']['events']['scripted']['unserved'], 1)
1058          early = [r for r in dec if r['offered'] < 1.8]
1059          self.assertTrue(early and max(r['started'] - r['offered'] for r in early) < 0.15)

1062  if __name__ == '__main__':
1063      unittest.main(verbosity=1)

D3 BEGIN benchmark/mind_picker/b5_mission/README.md (84 lines)
   1  # MIND-B5 — 30-minute motors-off mission: sustainable picker rate with camera, YOLO and heat pauses
   2  
   3  Research only. With RobotCam + YOLO running (CAM1-MID, #136) and the deployed adaptive heat pause in force (37 °C
   4  pause / 35 °C resume, #135), how many B2 V3 picker decisions does the phone serve in 30 minutes, how fast after an
   5  event, how much time do heat pauses take, and does throttling change the picker's choices? Timings count only from
   6  the owner's `oneshot.sh` run in native Termux with no agent resident.
   7  
   8  - **Motors off.** `motors.py` and pyserial are never imported (`main.py` imports neither; `run_mission.py`, which
   9    imports `motors` without `--dry`, is not imported); `main.Robot(None)` has no motors (distance 999 cm, moves are
  10    sleeps). An arm refuses to start, and is INVALID, if `motors` or `serial` is in `sys.modules` at its start or end.
  11    No serial port, no ESP32, no MTP, no memory notes, no vision calls.
  12  - **Setup per arm (not timed).** `main.get_detector(pinned=True)` first (run_mission's order); B4's server
  13    (`b4.server_command(<run>, 'P')`: b11716, setup_q4 flags, `--slot-save-path <run>/slots`, Q8_0 projector at 70
  14    image tokens; `bc.PINS`); the 40 B2 TEST prompts (V3 FWD, `b2.preflight`), the common prefix sent once and slot 0
  15    saved (`b4.warm`); `heat_control.HeatControl(HeatMonitor())` started (its own 1 s / 5 s root readings: essential
  16    safety monitoring; trace in the run folder); `robot.start_camera(heat)`; start reading; B4's 30 s watcher.
  17  - **Mission loop** = `Robot.run_mission` motors-off: heat transitions exactly as `main.py` (picker forbidden, camera
  18    stopped and shown stopped, `heat.enter`, then the pause-start reading; resume: `start_camera(heat)`,
  19    `heat.enter('ACTIVE', since=before the start)`, picker allowed with a fresh SEVERE check, then the pause-end
  20    reading; a battery stop or an error in that reading forbids the picker at once and ends the arm); HOLD sleeps 1 s,
  21    PAUSE the move time; ACTIVE runs B4's camera cycle (new frame, SizePolicy, detect) then the move time. Picker
  22    allowed only while ACTIVE and Android status < SEVERE; a forbid cancels a running decision (checked before and
  23    after every HTTP request and before its result is accepted; a forbid followed by an allow still cancels). Heat
  24    END, a camera start/stop failure or an unusable frame ends the arm.
  25  - **Start barrier.** The scheduler thread starts only after the mission loop's first heat / SEVERE assessment has
  26    set the picker's permission, so no 30 s mark is consumed before it.
  27  - **Pause accounting.** Each heat transition logs an `intent` row when it begins and a `done` row when it completed.
  28    A pause runs from the intent to leave ACTIVE (picker forbidden, camera stop begins) to the `done` re-entry into
  29    ACTIVE (camera started); only that counts as a resume, a failed restart does not (the pause stays open to the arm
  30    end). Camera stop, the pause-start reading and camera start count as pause time; the pause-end reading counts as
  31    ACTIVE (camera on, picker allowed). ACTIVE share = 1 − pause time / mission time.
  32  - **Events.** Scripted: the 40 B2 TEST items in id order, cycled, one every 10 s (180 per arm). Real: the most
  33    confident person's area bucket per ACTIVE cycle; appear / leave / bucket change → an event line in the B2 state
  34    format (mission M1, current activity exploring, memory none, all five actions; served, unscored). Pending queue of
  35    one: a newer event replaces an unserved one (dropped).
  36  - **Arms** (30 min of mission time each, order A then B): **A FIXED** at each 30 s mark, if allowed and idle, decide
  37    on the pending event; **B ADAPTIVE** as soon as an event is pending, the previous decision finished, the picker is
  38    allowed and the heat control's latest VIRTUAL-SKIN < 36.0 °C. A decision: slot 0 restored before every 5th, then
  39    `b2.score` unchanged (a real event's prompt is rendered first, inside its wall). A cancelled decision's event goes
  40    back to pending unless a newer one is waiting. The scheduler polls every 0.1 s (latency resolution).
  41  - **Deadline.** Nothing starts after it: the deadline is rechecked right before each camera cycle, so a transition
  42    (and its reading) that crosses it finishes but admits no cycle; the decision or camera cycle in flight finishes;
  43    overrun ≤ 15 s. A person change seen by a cycle admitted before the deadline that ends after it is not offered
  44    (counted `late_not_offered`).
  45  - **Readings** (`b4.reading`, per-PID memory checked): start (taken before the mission's t0: its relative time is
  46    negative), end, one at each heat-pause start (after the camera stop) and end (after the camera start). RobotCam
  47    memory required while the camera is on.
  48  - **Re-pin** (owner decision 2026-11-15, after the INVALID timed arm A of `20261115T002824Z`, whose llama-server
  49    threads were on cores 0–7 from the first scan). At arm start, at every camera start and whenever a watcher scan
  50    finds drift, every llama-server thread is re-pinned to 4–7 (`os.sched_setaffinity` per thread) and the detector to
  51    4–5 (its `check_affinity`, as `main.py`; at camera start `main.py`'s own re-pin is recorded). Every re-pin is
  52    recorded (time, trigger, process, threads, before/after) in the arm record and summarised in the report. Each scan
  53    records `/proc/<pid>/cpuset` and `cgroup` of llama-server, the runner and the detector caller thread, to show
  54    whether Android moved Termux between CPU groups. Drift corrected by one re-pin is recorded, not INVALID; a failed
  55    re-pin, or the same process found drifted in two consecutive scans, is INVALID.
  56  - **Gates.** Before every arm: cores 4–7, agent check, MEM-1 skin gate (launch-start skin + 1.0 °C, wait ≤ 30 min),
  57    status < 3, battery < 45 °C and ≥ 30 %. A timed run needs battery ≥ 85 % at its first start and ≥ 50 % at each
  58    `--resume`. Battery < 30 % at any reading ends the arm (INVALID) and the session.
  59  - **Labels.** Arm VALID / INVALID by B4's rules (watcher hit other than corrected drift, cores, missing or
  60    incomplete reading, camera failure, agent, request errors, overrun > 15 s) plus heat END and battery stop; an arm
  61    refused before any workload has the same record schema (empty metrics); an INVALID arm ends the session and is
  62    redone with `--resume`. A vs B descriptive (no matching claim). Energy INDICATIVE.
  63  - **Metrics per arm:** decisions served; offered / served / dropped / cancelled / unserved (scripted and real; real
  64    also late_not_offered); event→decision latency and decision wall median / p95; A's ticks; heat pauses and resumes,
  65    time to the first pause, total pause time, ACTIVE share; skin start / max (readings and heat trace) / end; minimum
  66    CPU caps at readings; camera cycle period (within one ACTIVE slot), detect ms 320 / 640; MemAvailable minimum;
  67    energy; agreement of served scripted choices with the B2 run of record (`b2_rules/runs/20261102T143251Z`, TEST V3
  68    FWD, SHA-256 pinned).
  69  - **Frozen definition:** SHA-256 of `oneshot.sh`, the run of record, the TEST set, every imported module under the
  70    repo, and the server command; `--resume` refuses any difference.
  71  - **Run** (native Termux, no agent): `bash ~/robot/benchmark/mind_picker/b5_mission/oneshot.sh`, then `… oneshot.sh
  72    --resume <UTC>` until `all arms done`. `--smoke`: each arm 180 s, gate before the first arm only, agent/cores
  73    checks recorded but not enforced, SMOKE - NOT A MEASUREMENT. Smoke only: the deployed HeatControl runs with
  74    lowered thresholds set per arm from that arm's start reading (after server load and camera start): pause at arm-
  75    start skin + 1.0 °C, resume at pause − 0.5 °C; its 60 s active / 30 s pause rules unchanged; set at runtime,
  76    `heat_control.py` unchanged. The smoke report gives each arm's thresholds and its pauses (camera stop) and resumes
  77    (camera start), and says when no pause or no resume happened. Timed runs use 37/35 °C. `oneshot.sh` is B4's
  78    launcher with only its paths, runner name and header changed (a shell script cannot import B4's).
  79  - Writes `~/robot_runs/mind_b5/<UTC>/`: run.json, sessionNN.json, sessionNN_end.json, arms.jsonl, requests.jsonl,
  80    NN_<arm>_server.log, heat_trace_<UTC>.jsonl, slots/, report.txt, artifacts.sha256.
  81  - Offline tests: `python -B test_b5.py` (fakes and a fake clock; one short real-time arm with the deployed
  82    HeatControl over a fake monitor; imports B4's test fixtures).
  83  - Known limits: battery % is seen only at readings (no extra sampler); a person flickering between frames produces
  84    several real events (the queue of one absorbs them); B4's carried-over interrupt/cleanup limitation.
END

D4 BEGIN benchmark/mind_picker/b5_mission/oneshot.sh (143 lines)
   1  #!/data/data/com.termux/files/usr/bin/bash
   2  # MIND-B5: 30-min motors-off mission (RobotCam+YOLO, heat pause, V3 picker arms A, B); no motors, serial port or
   3  # ESP32. Start from NATIVE Termux (prompt "~ $"), no agent running, charger unplugged, battery >= 85 %, Termux in
   4  # front, screen on:
   5  #   bash ~/robot/benchmark/mind_picker/b5_mission/oneshot.sh                  (session 1: a new run)
   6  #   bash ~/robot/benchmark/mind_picker/b5_mission/oneshot.sh --resume <UTC>   (next session: remaining arms)
   7  #   bash ~/robot/benchmark/mind_picker/b5_mission/oneshot.sh --smoke          (180 s arms, NOT A MEASUREMENT;
   8  #                                                                               an agent may stay resident)
   9  # B4's oneshot.sh (itself MEM-1's) with this folder's runner: same root, battery, lock, screen-timeout and signal
  10  # handling, the same 15 s root thermal logger (pinned to cores 0-3 at nice 10; the skin gate reads its log).
  11  # Root commands that call Android services use the DECISIONS #123 form:
  12  # su -c "<cmd> </dev/null >/data/local/tmp/<file> 2>&1", then the file is read.
  13  H=/data/data/com.termux/files/home
  14  L=$H/robot_runs/mind_b5
  15  R=$H/robot/benchmark/mind_picker/b5_mission
  16  S=/data/local/tmp/mind_b5_screen.txt
  17  mkdir -p "$L"
  18  CONSOLE=$L/oneshot_console_$(date -u +%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ).log
  19  say() { echo "[oneshot] $*" | tee -a "$CONSOLE"; }
  20  
  21  if [ -d /termux-home ] || [ "${PREFIX:-}" != /data/data/com.termux/files/usr ]; then
  22    echo "Run this from native Termux (~ \$), not from Debian."; exit 1
  23  fi
  24  su -c id >/dev/null 2>&1 || { echo "No root: su failed. Check Magisk."; exit 1; }
  25  [ -f "$R/b5.py" ] || { echo "Missing $R/b5.py"; exit 1; }
  26  case "$*" in
  27    ""|"--smoke"|"--resume "[0-9][0-9][0-9][0-9][0-9][0-9][0-9][0-9]T[0-9][0-9][0-9][0-9][0-9][0-9]Z) ;;
  28    *) echo "Usage: oneshot.sh [--smoke | --resume <UTC>]"; exit 1;;
  29  esac
  30  if pgrep -fa 'claude|agy|node|codex' >/dev/null; then
  31    if [ "$*" = --smoke ]; then
  32      echo "[oneshot] agent running (allowed for --smoke only; NOT A MEASUREMENT):"; pgrep -fa 'claude|agy|node|codex'
  33    else
  34      echo "An agent is still running:"; pgrep -fa 'claude|agy|node|codex'; echo "Quit it, then rerun."; exit 1
  35    fi
  36  fi
  37  if pgrep -fa 'llama-server|chat\.py' >/dev/null; then
  38    echo "llama-server or chat.py is already running:"; pgrep -fa 'llama-server|chat\.py'; echo "Stop it, then rerun."; exit 1
  39  fi
  40  RUNNERS='b4\.py|bc\.py|bv\.py|b[0-9]b?\.py|mem1\.py|chat_mtp\.py|repro\.py|vis3d\.py|power_map\.py|coresidency\.py|thermal_char\.py|vis[0-9]_run\.py|run_mission\.py|main\.py'
  41  if pgrep -fa "$RUNNERS" >/dev/null; then
  42    echo "A benchmark runner or the robot is already running:"; pgrep -fa "$RUNNERS"; echo "Stop it, then rerun."; exit 1
  43  fi
  44  check_battery() {  # checked at start and again right before the runner
  45    BAT=$(su -c 'cat /sys/class/power_supply/battery/status' 2>/dev/null)
  46    if [ "$BAT" != Discharging ]; then
  47      echo "[oneshot] Battery status is '$BAT', not Discharging: unplug the charger (power numbers would be invalid)." | tee -a "$CONSOLE"
  48      exit 1
  49    fi
  50  }
  51  cleanup() {
  52    [ -n "$LOCKED" ] || return 0  # refused before taking the lock: another run's files are not ours to touch
  53    touch "$L/.stop_thermal"
  54    if [ "$SCREEN_SET" = 1 ] && [[ $OLD_TIMEOUT =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]]; then
  55      su -c "settings put system screen_off_timeout $OLD_TIMEOUT </dev/null >$S 2>&1"
  56      su -c "settings get system screen_off_timeout </dev/null >$S 2>&1"
  57      BACK=$(su -c "cat $S" 2>/dev/null | tr -d '[:space:]')
  58      if [ "$BACK" = "$OLD_TIMEOUT" ]; then
  59        say "screen timeout restored to $OLD_TIMEOUT ms; read back: $BACK"
  60      else
  61        say "WARNING: screen timeout NOT restored (read back '$BACK', expected $OLD_TIMEOUT). Run in native Termux:"
  62        say "  su -c \"settings put system screen_off_timeout $OLD_TIMEOUT </dev/null >$S 2>&1\""
  63        RESTORE_FAILED=1
  64      fi
  65    fi
  66    [ -z "${PAUSE:-}" ] || kill "$PAUSE" 2>/dev/null
  67    termux-wake-unlock
  68    # the lock goes only once the logger has exited (it polls .stop_thermal every 5 s):
  69    # otherwise a new run could remove .stop_thermal under it and share its files with it
  70    JOBS="${LOGGER:-}"
  71    for i in $(seq 40); do
  72      ALIVE=; for p in $JOBS; do kill -0 "$p" 2>/dev/null && ALIVE=1; done
  73      [ -z "$ALIVE" ] && break
  74      sleep 0.5
  75    done
  76    if [ -n "$ALIVE" ]; then
  77      say "WARNING: logger still running after 20 s; $L/.lock kept. Remove it when they have stopped."
  78      RESTORE_FAILED=1
  79    else
  80      say "stopped logger"
  81      rm -rf "$L/.lock"
  82    fi
  83    [ -z "${RESTORE_FAILED:-}" ] || exit 1
  84  }
  85  LOCKED=
  86  SCREEN_SET=0
  87  trap cleanup EXIT
  88  RUNNER=
  89  LAUNCHING=
  90  PENDING=
  91  on_signal() {  # before the runner starts: exit (the EXIT trap cleans up); after: forward it and keep waiting
  92    if [ -n "$RUNNER" ]; then kill -TERM "$RUNNER" 2>/dev/null
  93    elif [ -n "$LAUNCHING" ]; then PENDING=1  # between `&` and RUNNER=$!: forwarded right after
  94    else exit "$1"; fi
  95  }
  96  trap 'on_signal 143' TERM; trap 'on_signal 129' HUP; trap 'on_signal 130' INT
  97  # one run at a time: runs share thermal.log, .stop_thermal and port 8080
  98  if ! mkdir "$L/.lock" 2>/dev/null; then
  99    echo "Another MIND-B5 launcher holds $L/.lock (pid $(cat "$L/.lock/pid" 2>/dev/null))."
 100    echo "If no run is active (for example after a crash), remove it: rm -r $L/.lock"; exit 1
 101  fi
 102  LOCKED=1
 103  echo $$ > "$L/.lock/pid"
 104  check_battery
 105  
 106  termux-wake-lock
 107  pause() { sleep "$1" & PAUSE=$!; wait "$PAUSE"; PAUSE=; }  # a trapped signal interrupts wait, not a foreground sleep
 108  # keep the screen on (Android moves Termux off the big cores when it is not in front)
 109  OLD_TIMEOUT=
 110  if su -c "settings get system screen_off_timeout </dev/null >$S 2>&1"; then
 111    OLD_TIMEOUT=$(su -c "cat $S" 2>/dev/null | tr -d '[:space:]')
 112  fi
 113  if ! [[ $OLD_TIMEOUT =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]]; then
 114    say "Could not read the screen timeout (read '$OLD_TIMEOUT': $(su -c "cat $S" 2>/dev/null | head -c 200)); not started."; exit 1
 115  fi
 116  SCREEN_SET=1  # from here on, cleanup restores the validated old value
 117  su -c "settings put system screen_off_timeout 2147483647 </dev/null >$S 2>&1"
 118  su -c "settings get system screen_off_timeout </dev/null >$S 2>&1"
 119  NOW=$(su -c "cat $S" 2>/dev/null | tr -d '[:space:]')
 120  if [ "$NOW" != 2147483647 ]; then
 121    say "Could not set the screen timeout (read back '$NOW'); not started."; exit 1
 122  fi
 123  say "screen timeout was $OLD_TIMEOUT ms; set to 2147483647 for the run (read back)"
 124  [ "$OLD_TIMEOUT" = 2147483647 ] && say "WARNING: the saved timeout is already 2147483647 (left by an earlier run?); set it yourself afterwards"
 125  rm -f "$L/.stop_thermal" "$L/thermal_logger_sched.txt"
 126  
 127  su -c "taskset -p f \$\$ >/dev/null 2>&1; renice -n 10 -p \$\$ >/dev/null 2>&1; { grep Cpus_allowed_list /proc/\$\$/status; echo nice=\$(cut -d' ' -f19 /proc/\$\$/stat); } > $L/thermal_logger_sched.txt 2>&1; while [ ! -f $L/.stop_thermal ]; do echo \"\$(date -u +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ) z9=\$(cat /sys/class/thermal/thermal_zone9/temp) z10=\$(cat /sys/class/thermal/thermal_zone10/temp) z11=\$(cat /sys/class/thermal/thermal_zone11/temp) CPU-core_reading,_not_a_heat_state=1\" >> $L/thermal.log; sleep 15; done" &
 128  LOGGER=$!
 129  
 130  pause 6
 131  say "CPU-core reading, not a heat state: $(tail -1 "$L/thermal.log")"
 132  check_battery
 133  
 134  # The runner runs as a job so TERM/HUP/INT sent to this launcher reach it: it stops llama-server
 135  # itself, and we wait for that before the EXIT trap restores the screen timeout.
 136  LAUNCHING=1
 137  env PYTHONUNBUFFERED=1 python -u "$R/b5.py" "$@" > >(tee -a "$CONSOLE") 2>&1 &
 138  RUNNER=$!
 139  [ -z "$PENDING" ] || kill -TERM "$RUNNER" 2>/dev/null
 140  while kill -0 "$RUNNER" 2>/dev/null; do wait "$RUNNER"; done
 141  wait "$RUNNER"; RC=$?
 142  if [ "$RC" = 0 ]; then say "done; console log: $CONSOLE"; else say "RUNNER FAILED (exit $RC); console log: $CONSOLE"; fi
 143  exit "$RC"
END

 # ---------------------------------------------------------------- one arm
 
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------- re-pin (owner decision 2026-11-15)
+
+class Repinner:
+    """Re-pins like a robot would: every llama-server thread to SERVER_CPUS (os.sched_setaffinity per thread) and the
+    detector to 4-5 (its own check_affinity, as main.py). Every re-pin is recorded in events (mono, trigger, process,
+    threads, before/after, ok). Called from the mission thread and the watcher thread (lock)."""
+
+    def __init__(self, targets):
+        self.targets, self.events, self.lock = targets, [], threading.Lock()
+
+    def add(self, ev):
+        with self.lock:
+            self.events.append({'mono': round(time.monotonic(), 3), **ev})
+        return self.events[-1]
+
+    def server(self, trigger):
+        pid = self.targets.get('server_pid')
+        before, after, errors = {}, {}, {}
+        try:
+            tids = sorted(int(t.name) for t in (b4.PROC / str(pid) / 'task').iterdir())
+        except OSError as e:
+            return self.add({'trigger': trigger, 'process': 'llama_server', 'ok': False, 'errors': {'task': str(e)}})
+        for tid in tids:
+            try:
+                before[tid] = sorted(os.sched_getaffinity(tid))
+                if set(before[tid]) != b4.SERVER_CPUS:
+                    os.sched_setaffinity(tid, b4.SERVER_CPUS)
+                    after[tid] = sorted(os.sched_getaffinity(tid))
+                    if set(after[tid]) != b4.SERVER_CPUS:
+                        errors[tid] = f'after {after[tid]}'
+            except ProcessLookupError:
+                before.pop(tid, None)  # the thread ended: nothing to pin
+            except OSError as e:
+                errors[tid] = f'{type(e).__name__}: {e}'
+        return self.add({'trigger': trigger, 'process': 'llama_server', 'threads': sorted(after),
+                         'before': before, 'after': after, 'ok': not errors, 'errors': errors})
+
+    def detector(self, trigger, result=None):
+        """result: a check_affinity() result already obtained (main.py's at camera start); else one is run now."""
+        r = result if result is not None else self.targets['detector'].check_affinity()
+        return self.add({'trigger': trigger, 'process': 'detector', 'threads': r['repinned'], 'before': r['before'],
+                         'after': r['after'], 'ok': r['ok'], 'errors': r['errors']})
+
+
+DRIFT = {'llama_server': re.compile(r'llama-server thread \d+ Cpus_allowed_list'),
+         'detector': re.compile(r'detector threads \{')}
+
+
+def groups(targets):
+    """/proc/<pid>/cpuset and cgroup of llama-server, the runner and the detector caller thread (error text when
+    unreadable): shows whether Android moved Termux between CPU groups."""
+    paths = {'runner': b4.PROC / str(os.getpid())}
+    if targets.get('server_pid') is not None:
+        paths['llama_server'] = b4.PROC / str(targets['server_pid'])
+    if targets.get('detector') is not None:
+        paths['detector'] = b4.PROC / str(os.getpid()) / 'task' / str(targets['detector'].caller_tid)
+    out = {}
+    for name, p in paths.items():
+        for f in ('cpuset', 'cgroup'):
+            try:
+                out[f'{name}_{f}'] = (p / f).read_text().strip()
+            except OSError as e:
+                out[f'{name}_{f}'] = f'{type(e).__name__}: {e}'
+    return out
+
+
+def scan(targets, rp):
+    """b4.watch_check, then drift (a llama-server thread off 4-7, a detector thread off 4-5) is split from the other
+    hits and re-pinned at once; the CPU groups are recorded."""
+    x = b4.watch_check(targets)
+    hits, drift = [], {}
+    for h in x['hits']:
+        p = next((k for k, rx in DRIFT.items() if rx.match(h)), None)
+        (drift.setdefault(p, []) if p else hits).append(h)
+    x.update(hits=hits, drift=drift, groups=groups(targets))
+    x['repins'] = [rp.server('watch') if p == 'llama_server' else rp.detector('watch') for p in sorted(drift)]
+    return x
+
+
+def watch_problems(w, repins):
+    """b4's watcher rules, except that drift corrected by one re-pin is recorded, not a problem; INVALID when any
+    re-pin fails or the same process is drifted in two consecutive scans."""
+    why = b4.thread_problems(w, 'watch')
+    why += [f'watch: {h}' for x in w['samples'] for h in x['hits']]
+    why += [f're-pin failed ({e["trigger"]}, {e["process"]}): {e.get("errors")}' for e in repins if not e['ok']]
+    for a, b in zip(w['samples'], w['samples'][1:]):
+        why += [f'watch: {p} drifted in two consecutive scans ({a["mono"]}, {b["mono"]})'
+                for p in sorted(set(a.get('drift', {})) & set(b.get('drift', {})))]
+    return why if w['samples'] else why + ['watch: no scan']
+
+
+def record_camera_repins(heat, rp):
+    """Records main.py's detector re-pin at every camera start: start_camera passes its check_affinity() result to
+    heat.event('detector_affinity', ...); this instance wrapper also hands it to rp (heat_control.py unchanged)."""
+    event = heat.event
+
+    def wrapped(name, detail=None, dump=None):
+        if name == 'detector_affinity':
+            rp.detector('camera_start', detail)
+        return event(name, detail, dump)
+    heat.event = wrapped
+
+
 def need(c):
     return ['runner', 'llama_server'] + (['robotcam'] if c['camera'] else [])

+def repin_summary(rec):
+    """Re-pins that changed threads or failed, per trigger and process; drifted scans per process; every CPU group
+    (cpuset / cgroup) value seen per process across the scans (more than one value: Android moved it)."""
+    samples = rec.get('watch', {}).get('samples', [])
+    acted = {}
+    for e in rec.get('repins', []):
+        if e.get('threads') or not e['ok']:
+            k = f'{e["trigger"]}/{e["process"]}'
+            acted[k] = acted.get(k, 0) + 1
+    drift = {}
+    for x in samples:
+        for p in x.get('drift', {}):
+            drift[p] = drift.get(p, 0) + 1
+    seen = {}
+    for x in samples:
+        for k, v in x.get('groups', {}).items():
+            seen.setdefault(k, set()).add(v)
+    return {'acted': acted, 'failed': sum(not e['ok'] for e in rec.get('repins', [])), 'drifted_scans': drift,
+            'groups_seen': {k: sorted(v) for k, v in seen.items()}}
