+class Repin(unittest.TestCase):
+    """Owner decision 2026-11-15: re-pin like a robot; drift corrected by one re-pin is VALID and recorded."""
+
+    def setUp(self):
+        self.d = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
+        self.proc = Path(self.d.name)
+        for tid in (11, 12, 13):
+            (self.proc / '4242' / 'task' / str(tid)).mkdir(parents=True)
+        self.aff = {11: {0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7}, 12: {4, 5, 6, 7}, 13: {0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7}}
+        self.patches = [unittest.mock.patch.object(b4, 'PROC', self.proc),
+                        unittest.mock.patch.object(b5.os, 'sched_getaffinity', self.get),
+                        unittest.mock.patch.object(b5.os, 'sched_setaffinity', self.set)]
+        for p in self.patches:
+            p.start()
+        self.fail = set()
+
+    def tearDown(self):
+        for p in self.patches:
+            p.stop()
+        self.d.cleanup()
+
+    def get(self, tid):
+        if tid not in self.aff:
+            raise ProcessLookupError(tid)
+        return set(self.aff[tid])
+
+    def set(self, tid, cpus):
+        if tid in self.fail:
+            raise PermissionError('EPERM')
+        self.aff[tid] = set(cpus)
+
+    def test_server_repin_records_before_after(self):
+        rp = b5.Repinner({'server_pid': 4242})
+        e = rp.server('watch')
+        self.assertTrue(e['ok'])
+        self.assertEqual((e['threads'], e['before'][11], e['after'][11]), ([11, 13], [0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7],
+                                                                           [4, 5, 6, 7]))
+        self.assertEqual({t: sorted(c) for t, c in self.aff.items()}, {t: [4, 5, 6, 7] for t in (11, 12, 13)})
+        self.assertEqual(rp.events, [e])
+
+    def test_server_repin_failure_and_vanished_thread(self):

+        self.assertFalse(e['ok'])

+        self.assertEqual(x, {'acted': {'watch/llama_server': 1}, 'failed': 0, 'drifted_scans': {'llama_server': 1},
+                             'groups_seen': {'llama_server_cpuset': ['/foreground', '/top-app']}})
+
+    def test_validity(self):
+        ok = {'trigger': 'watch', 'process': 'llama_server', 'ok': True}
+        bad = {**ok, 'ok': False, 'errors': {13: 'EPERM'}}
+        # drift corrected by one re-pin: VALID, the event recorded
+        rec = arm_record(watch=self.watch([[], ['llama_server'], [], ['detector'], []]), repins=[ok, ok])
+        self.assertEqual(b5.arm_problems(rec, [CYC]), [])
+        # the same process drifted in two consecutive scans: INVALID
+        rec = arm_record(watch=self.watch([[], ['llama_server'], ['llama_server'], []]), repins=[ok, ok])
+        self.assertTrue(any('two consecutive scans' in p for p in b5.arm_problems(rec, [CYC])))
+        # different processes in consecutive scans: each corrected once, VALID
+        rec = arm_record(watch=self.watch([['llama_server'], ['detector']]), repins=[ok, ok])
+        self.assertEqual(b5.arm_problems(rec, [CYC]), [])
+        # a failed re-pin (watch, arm start or camera start): INVALID
+        for trig in ('watch', 'arm_start', 'camera_start'):
+            rec = arm_record(watch=self.watch([[]]), repins=[{**bad, 'trigger': trig}])
+            self.assertTrue(any('re-pin failed' in p for p in b5.arm_problems(rec, [CYC])), trig)
+        rec = arm_record(repins=[bad])
+        del rec['watch']
+        self.assertTrue(any('re-pin failed' in p for p in b5.arm_problems(rec, [CYC])))
+

BEGIN benchmark/mind_picker/b4_baseline/b4.py (1031 lines, excerpt lines 292-329)
 292  def watch_check(targets):
 293      """One light /proc scan: agent processes, the runner's cores 4-7, the affinity of every llama-server thread
 294      (targets['server_pid'], PROC/<pid>/task/*) 4-7, the detector threads' (targets['detector']) 4-5; an unreadable
 295      thread list, or a thread that exists but cannot be read, is a hit; a thread that disappeared between the listing
 296      and its read (FileNotFoundError / ProcessLookupError) is skipped and counted in 'vanished', not a hit.
 297      Returns {'mono', 'hits', 'vanished'} (no hits: clean)."""
 298      hits, vanished = [], 0
 299      for p in Path('/proc').iterdir():
 300          if p.name.isdigit():
 301              try:
 302                  cmd = (p / 'cmdline').read_bytes().replace(b'\0', b' ').decode(errors='replace')
 303              except OSError:
 304                  continue
 305              if AGENT_RE.search(cmd):
 306                  hits.append(f'agent pid {p.name}: {cmd[:100]}')
 307      if not cores_ok():
 308          hits.append(f'runner cores {sorted(cr.allowed_cpus())}')
 309      if (pid := targets.get('server_pid')) is not None:
 310          try:
 311              tids = sorted(t.name for t in (PROC / str(pid) / 'task').iterdir())
 312          except OSError as e:
 313              tids = []
 314              hits.append(f'llama-server threads unreadable: {e}')
 315          for tid in tids:  # affinity is per thread: every one, not only the leader
 316              try:
 317                  line = next(l for l in (PROC / str(pid) / 'task' / tid / 'status').read_text().splitlines()
 318                              if l.startswith('Cpus_allowed_list'))
 319                  if parse_cpus(line.split(':', 1)[1]) != SERVER_CPUS:
 320                      hits.append(f'llama-server thread {tid} {line}')
 321              except (FileNotFoundError, ProcessLookupError):
 322                  vanished += 1  # the thread ended after the listing
 323              except (OSError, StopIteration, ValueError) as e:
 324                  hits.append(f'llama-server thread {tid} affinity unreadable: {e}')
 325      if (det := targets.get('detector')) is not None:
 326          bad = {t: c for t, c in det.affinity().items() if c != DETECTOR_CPUS}
 327          if bad:
 328              hits.append(f'detector threads {bad}')
 329      return {'mono': round(time.monotonic(), 3), 'hits': hits, 'vanished': vanished}
END
BEGIN benchmark/mind_picker/b4_baseline/b4.py (1031 lines, excerpt lines 254-260)
 254  def thread_problems(s, name):
 255      why = [f'{name}: {s["error"]}'] if s['error'] else []
 256      why += [f'{name}: still running after its join'] if s['alive'] else []
 257      got = s.get('sched') or {}
 258      if got.get('cpus') != THREAD_CPUS or got.get('nice') != THREAD_NICE:
 259          why.append(f'{name}: got cpus {got.get("cpus")} nice {got.get("nice")}, need {THREAD_CPUS} nice {THREAD_NICE}')
 260      return why
END
BEGIN benchmark/mind_picker/b4_baseline/b4.py (1031 lines, excerpt lines 336-339)
 336  def watch_problems(w):
 337      why = thread_problems(w, 'watch')
 338      why += [f'watch: {h}' for x in w['samples'] for h in x['hits']]
 339      return why if w['samples'] else why + ['watch: no scan']
END
BEGIN benchmark/mind_picker/b4_baseline/b4.py (1031 lines, excerpt lines 216-246)
 216  class Periodic:
 217      """fn() at once, then every period s, and once more when stopped, from a thread asking for cores 0-3 at nice 10
 218      (mem1.pin_sampler; what it got is recorded). A slow call skips the missed slots."""
 219  
 220      def __init__(self, fn, period):
 221          self.fn, self.period, self.samples, self.sched, self.error = fn, period, [], None, None
 222          self.halt = threading.Event()
 223          self.thread = threading.Thread(target=self.loop, daemon=True)
 224  
 225      def start(self):
 226          self.thread.start()
 227          return self
 228  
 229      def loop(self):
 230          try:
 231              self.sched = mem1.pin_sampler()
 232              t0, last = time.monotonic(), False
 233              while True:
 234                  self.samples.append(self.fn())
 235                  if last:
 236                      return
 237                  k = int((time.monotonic() - t0) / self.period) + 1
 238                  last = self.halt.wait(max(0.0, t0 + k * self.period - time.monotonic()))
 239          except Exception as e:
 240              self.error = f'{type(e).__name__}: {e}'
 241  
 242      def stop(self):
 243          self.halt.set()
 244          self.thread.join(ROOT_TIMEOUT_S + 5)
 245          return {'period_s': self.period, 'sched': self.sched, 'error': self.error, 'alive': self.thread.is_alive(),
 246                  'samples': self.samples}
END
BEGIN benchmark/mind_picker/b4_baseline/b4.py (1031 lines, excerpt lines 163-193)
 163  def reading(pids=None, light=False, tag='b4_read'):
 164      """Skin, status, caps, battery (+ charge counter, voltage); unless light, MemAvailable and process memory. Every
 165      section's return code is kept (None: the section did not finish)."""
 166      c = {'utc': cr.utc(), 'mono': round(time.monotonic(), 3), 'caps': caps()}
 167      if not light:
 168          c['avail_kb'] = next(int(l.split()[1]) for l in Path('/proc/meminfo').read_text().splitlines()
 169                               if l.startswith('MemAvailable'))
 170      began = time.monotonic()
 171      try:
 172          rc, out = cr.root(root_script(pids or {}, light), tag, timeout=ROOT_TIMEOUT_S)
 173      except (OSError, subprocess.SubprocessError) as e:
 174          rc, out = None, ''
 175          c['root_error'] = f'{type(e).__name__}: {e}'[:300]
 176      c.update(root_rc=rc, root_s=round(time.monotonic() - began, 3))
 177      if rc != 0:
 178          c['root_stderr'] = cr.root_stderr()[:300]
 179      sec = mem1.sections(out)
 180      c['battery_rc'], text = sec.get('battery', (None, ''))
 181      try:
 182          pct, status, temp, charge, volt = text.split()[:5]
 183          c.update(battery_pct=int(pct), battery_status=status, battery_c=int(temp) / 10, charge_uah=int(charge),
 184                   voltage_uv=int(volt))
 185      except ValueError:
 186          c['battery_error'] = text[:300]
 187      c['dump_rc'], text = sec.get('thermalservice', (None, ''))
 188      d = cr.parse_dump(text)
 189      c.update(status=d['status'], skin=d['skin'])
 190      for name, (rc_m, text) in sec.items():
 191          if name.startswith('mem_'):
 192              c[name] = {**mem_section(text), 'rc': rc_m}
 193      return c
END
BEGIN benchmark/mind_picker/b4_baseline/b4.py (1031 lines, excerpt lines 564-581)
 564  def stop_camera(robot):
 565      """main.Robot.stop_camera(), then capture must show stopped (frame files gone) within CAMERA_STOP_S."""
 566      t0 = time.monotonic()
 567      out = {}
 568      try:  # metadata in its own step: a failure here never skips the stop below
 569          out['affinity_end'] = robot.detector.affinity() if robot.detector else None
 570      except Exception as e:
 571          out['affinity_error'] = f'detector affinity at stop: {type(e).__name__}: {e}'
 572      try:
 573          robot.stop_camera()
 574      except Exception as e:
 575          return {**out, 'error': f'stop_camera: {type(e).__name__}: {e}'}
 576      while time.monotonic() - t0 < CAMERA_STOP_S:
 577          status = read_frame()['status']
 578          if status == 'missing':
 579              return {**out, 'stopped_s': round(time.monotonic() - t0, 2)}
 580          time.sleep(0.2)
 581      return {**out, 'error': f'capture not shown stopped {CAMERA_STOP_S} s after stop_camera (status {status})'}
END

BEGIN main.py (690 lines, excerpt lines 222-254)
 222      def start_camera(self, heat=None):
 223          """heat: the mission's HeatControl, for the detector trace lines (None: no trace)."""
 224          created = self.detector is None
 225          # Shared by detect_scene and the robot loop. MID cores (#128); raises before `am start` when the
 226          # ORT workers or an affinity cannot be verified, so the mission does not start.
 227          self.detector = get_detector(pinned=True)
 228          if created and heat:
 229              heat.event('detector_config', {**self.detector.config(), 'camera_rate': CAMERA_RATE})
 230          # Affinity is a power setting, not a safety stop: a failed re-pin is logged and the mission continues.
 231          affinity = self.detector.check_affinity()
 232          if not affinity['ok']:
 233              print(f"[DETECTOR] affinity re-pin failed: {affinity['errors']} — continuing")
 234          if heat:
 235              heat.event('detector_affinity', affinity)
 236          self.slot = new_slot()
 237          self.slot_n += 1
 238          self.last_640 = None
 239          if self.camera_source == 'robotcam':
 240              self.camera_session = None
 241              self.camera_frame = None
 242              started_boot_s = time.clock_gettime(time.CLOCK_BOOTTIME)
 243              subprocess.run(['am', 'start', '-n', 'com.pixelrobot.robotcam/.StartActivity',
 244                              '--es', 'mode', 'B', '--ei', 'rate', str(CAMERA_RATE)],
 245                             check=True, timeout=10, stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL,
 246                             stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL)
 247              deadline = time.monotonic() + 8
 248              while time.monotonic() < deadline:
 249                  result = read_frame(min_capture_boot_s=started_boot_s)
 250                  if result['status'] == 'ok':
 251                      self.camera_session = result['session']
 252                      return
 253                  time.sleep(0.1)
 254              raise RuntimeError('RobotCam did not publish a usable frame')
END

BEGIN main.py (690 lines, excerpt lines 568-631)
 568      def run_mission(self, mission_text, heat=None):
 569          """Run until mission complete or stopped, under heat control (#127, #129).
 570          heat: a started heat_control.HeatControl; the camera is already on (ACTIVE)."""
 571          if heat is None:
 572              raise ValueError('run_mission needs heat control (heat_control.HeatControl)')
 573          self.mission = mission_text
 574          self.state = 'navigating'
 575          self.running = True
 576          self.heat = heat
 577          if self.picker:
 578              self.picker.allow()
 579          print(f'\n[ROBOT] Mission: {mission_text}')
 580  
 581          try:
 582              while self.running and self.state != 'found':
 583                  self.collect_pick()  # also in PAUSE/HOLD: every pick is traced when it ends
 584                  target, reason = heat.decide(self.get_distance())
 585                  if self.picker and self.severe_now():
 586                      self.picker.forbid()  # before this cycle's vision: no further picker request at SEVERE
 587                  self.heat_text = heat.summary()
 588                  if target == 'END':
 589                      print(f'[HEAT] END: {reason}')
 590                      break
 591                  if target != heat.phase:
 592                      print(f'[HEAT] {heat.phase} -> {target}: {reason}')
 593                      if heat.phase == 'ACTIVE':      # into PAUSE or HOLD: camera off, detector stays loaded
 594                          if self.motors: self.motors.stop()
 595                          if self.picker: self.picker.forbid()  # no M2 or Gemma outside ACTIVE (#129)
 596                          try:
 597                              self.stop_camera()
 598                          except Exception as exc:
 599                              print(f'[CAM] stop failed: {exc} — mission ends')
 600                              heat.event('camera_stop_fail', str(exc))
 601                              break
 602                          heat.event('camera_stop_ok')
 603                          self.end_slot(heat)
 604                          heat.enter(target, since=heat.now())
 605                      elif target == 'ACTIVE':
 606                          started = heat.now()
 607                          try:
 608                              self.start_camera(heat)  # resets the frame counter, session and slot numbers
 609                          except Exception as exc:
 610                              print(f'[CAM] start failed: {exc} — mission ends')
 611                              heat.event('camera_start_fail', str(exc))
 612                              break
 613                          heat.event('camera_start_ok')
 614                          heat.enter('ACTIVE', since=started)
 615                          if self.picker: self.picker.allow()
 616                      else:                           # PAUSE <-> HOLD; camera already off
 617                          heat.enter(target)
 618  
 619                  if heat.phase == 'HOLD':
 620                      if self.motors: self.motors.stop()
 621                      time.sleep(1)
 622                      continue
 623                  move = self.run_cycle() if heat.phase == 'ACTIVE' else self.pause_cycle()
 624  
 625                  if move == 'STOP':
 626                      break
 627          finally:                                # also on Ctrl-C, SIGTERM/SIGHUP (SystemExit) and errors
 628              if heat.phase == 'ACTIVE':
 629                  self.end_slot(heat)
 630          print('[ROBOT] Mission ended')
 631          if self.motors: self.motors.stop()
END
BEGIN picker.py (257 lines, excerpt lines 158-257)
 158  class Picker:
 159      """One background worker thread runs each pick: M2 (relate.Relate) on the latest 640 frame, then Gemma. It gets
 160      plain data in a request and returns a result; it never touches motors, camera, heat state or the robot. Any
 161      failure becomes an EXPLORE result with the reason (a fallback, never a stop). The mission thread calls allow()
 162      while ACTIVE and below SEVERE and forbid() otherwise; a running pick checks before M2, after M2 and before every
 163      HTTP request, and a forbid() cancels it for good (a later allow() does not revive it). Only a request already
 164      sent (<= TIMEOUT_S) or an M2 inference already started can still finish."""
 165  
 166      def __init__(self, gemma, relate):
 167          self.gemma, self.relate = gemma, relate
 168          self.requests, self.results = queue.Queue(), queue.Queue()
 169          self.allowed = threading.Event()
 170          self.generation = 0  # forbid() bumps it; a pick runs only while it is unchanged
 171          ready = queue.Queue()
 172          self.thread = threading.Thread(target=self.loop, args=(ready,), daemon=True)
 173          self.thread.start()
 174          error = ready.get()
 175          if error:
 176              self.thread.join()
 177              raise error
 178  
 179      def loop(self, ready):
 180          try:
 181              self.relate.pin_caller()  # this thread runs M2: BIG 6-7, verified
 182          except BaseException as e:
 183              ready.put(e)
 184              return
 185          ready.put(None)
 186          while (request := self.requests.get()) is not None:
 187              self.results.put(self.pick(request))
 188  
 189      def allow(self):
 190          self.allowed.set()
 191  
 192      def forbid(self):
 193          if self.allowed.is_set():
 194              self.generation += 1
 195              self.allowed.clear()
 196  
 197      def pick(self, request):
 198          out = {'slot': request['slot'], 'trigger': request['trigger'],
 199                 'eligible': [MODES[m] for m in request['eligible']], 'mode': 'EXPLORE', 'probs': None,
 200                 'm2_ms': None, 'm2_boxes': None, 'm2_relations': None, 'm2_repinned': None,
 201                 'picker_ms': None, 'prompt_tokens': None, 'fallback': None, 'cancelled': False}
 202          generation = self.generation
 203  
 204          def check():
 205              if generation != self.generation or not self.allowed.is_set():
 206                  raise Cancelled('optional work not allowed (left ACTIVE, SEVERE or stop)')
 207          self.gemma.check = check
 208          stage = 'M2'
 209          try:
 210              check()
 211              affinity = self.relate.check_affinity()
 212              out['m2_repinned'] = affinity['repinned']
 213              if not affinity['ok']:
 214                  raise RuntimeError(f"affinity: {affinity['errors']}")
 215              check()  # a forbid() during the affinity check: no M2 run
 216              text, out['m2_boxes'], out['m2_relations'], out['m2_ms'] = self.relate.relations(*request['frame_640'])
 217              check()
 218              stage = 'Gemma'
 219              phrases = out['eligible']
 220              started = time.perf_counter()
 221              dist = self.gemma.decide(request['situation'] + '\n' + text, phrases)
 222              check()  # a forbid() while /completion was in flight cancels it too
 223              out['picker_ms'] = (time.perf_counter() - started) * 1000
 224              out['probs'] = dist
 225              out['prompt_tokens'] = self.gemma.prompt_tokens
 226              choice = max(phrases, key=lambda k: dist[k])  # ties: first offered, as in ladder.py
 227              out['mode'] = request['eligible'][phrases.index(choice)]
 228          except Exception as e:
 229              out['mode'] = 'EXPLORE'
 230              out['fallback'] = f'{stage}: {type(e).__name__}: {e}'
 231              out['cancelled'] = isinstance(e, Cancelled)
 232          return out
 233  
 234      def submit(self, request):
 235          self.requests.put(request)
 236  
 237      def result(self):
 238          """A finished pick, or None (never waits)."""
 239          try:
 240              return self.results.get_nowait()
 241          except queue.Empty:
 242              return None
 243  
 244      def stop(self, timeout=TIMEOUT_S + 5):
 245          """Cancels the running pick (after its current request) and ends the worker; True when it has ended."""
 246          self.forbid()
 247          self.requests.put(None)
 248          self.thread.join(timeout)
 249          return not self.thread.is_alive()
 250  
 251      def config(self):
 252          return {'url': URL, 'options': list(MODES.values()), 'instruction': INSTRUCTION, 'timeout_s': TIMEOUT_S,
 253                  'cadence': {'events': ['person_appeared', 'person_left', 'blocked'], 'min_gap_s': MIN_GAP_S,
 254                              'heartbeat_s': HEARTBEAT_S, 'phases': ['ACTIVE'], 'not_at_status': 'SEVERE or above'},
 255                  'm2': {'cpus': sorted(self.relate.cpus), 'threads': 2, 'worker_tids': self.relate.worker_tids,
 256                         'caller_tid': self.relate.caller_tid, 'affinity': self.relate.affinity(),
 257                         'parity': 'graph SHA-256 equals the parity-passed graph (#130)'}}
END
BEGIN detect_person.py (283 lines, excerpt lines 72-77)
  72  def pin(tid, cpus):
  73      if tid in (0, os.getpid()):  # 0 = the caller, pid = the main thread: never pinned
  74          raise RuntimeError(f'refusing to pin tid {tid}')
  75      os.sched_setaffinity(tid, cpus)
  76      if os.sched_getaffinity(tid) != cpus:
  77          raise RuntimeError(f'thread {tid} affinity did not take effect')
END
BEGIN detect_person.py (283 lines, excerpt lines 66-67)
  66  def thread_ids():
  67      return {int(t) for t in os.listdir(f'/proc/{os.getpid()}/task')}
END
BEGIN detect_person.py (283 lines, excerpt lines 136-146)
 136      def affinity(self):
 137          """{tid: sorted cpus or error text} for the caller and workers; read only."""
 138          live, out = thread_ids(), {}
 139          for tid in [self.caller_tid] + self.worker_tids:
 140              try:
 141                  if tid not in live:  # never touch a tid that left this process (it could be reused elsewhere)
 142                      raise ProcessLookupError(f'thread {tid} is gone')
 143                  out[tid] = sorted(os.sched_getaffinity(tid))
 144              except OSError as e:
 145                  out[tid] = f'{type(e).__name__}: {e}'
 146          return out
END
BEGIN detect_person.py (283 lines, excerpt lines 148-160)
 148      def check_affinity(self):
 149          """Re-pin and verify any of the 3 threads whose affinity drifted (Android cpuset changes can reset it).
 150          Never raises: the result says ok or not."""
 151          before = self.affinity()
 152          repin = [tid for tid, cpus in before.items() if isinstance(cpus, list) and set(cpus) != self.cpus]
 153          errors = {tid: cpus for tid, cpus in before.items() if isinstance(cpus, str)}
 154          for tid in repin:
 155              try:
 156                  pin(tid, self.cpus)
 157              except (OSError, RuntimeError) as e:
 158                  errors[tid] = f'{type(e).__name__}: {e}'
 159          after = self.affinity() if repin else before
 160          return {'before': before, 'repinned': repin, 'after': after, 'ok': not errors, 'errors': errors}
END
BEGIN heat_control.py (345 lines, excerpt lines 244-273)
 244  def decide(now, readings, state):
 245      """(target, reason): target ACTIVE / PAUSE / HOLD / END. Pure; times are monotonic s.
 246      readings: {'dump': {'t', 'status', 'skin'} or None, 'fast': [{'t', 'cpu_c', 'bat_c'}, ...] oldest first}.
 247      state: {'phase': ACTIVE / PAUSE / HOLD, 'since': start of the phase (a HOLD keeps its pause's start)}.
 248      Staleness: a reading exactly SKIN_STALE_S / FAST_STALE_S old is still fresh; older ends the mission."""
 249      # Precedence and staleness copied from benchmark/coresidency/coresidency.py: block_limit(); fail-closed first.
 250      dump, fast = readings['dump'], readings['fast']
 251      if dump is None or now - dump['t'] > SKIN_STALE_S:
 252          return 'END', f'fail-closed: no Android status + {SKIN} reading for > {SKIN_STALE_S} s'
 253      if not fast or now - fast[-1]['t'] > FAST_STALE_S:
 254          return 'END', f'fail-closed: no CPU zone + battery reading for > {FAST_STALE_S} s'
 255      if fast[-1]['bat_c'] >= BATTERY_STOP_C:
 256          return 'END', f'battery {fast[-1]["bat_c"]:.1f} >= {BATTERY_STOP_C} degC'
 257      if len(fast) >= CPU_FAULT_N and all(s['cpu_c'] >= CPU_FAULT_C for s in fast[-CPU_FAULT_N:]):
 258          return 'END', (f'CPU zone >= {CPU_FAULT_C:.0f} degC in {CPU_FAULT_N} consecutive 1 s samples '
 259                         f'({[s["cpu_c"] for s in fast[-CPU_FAULT_N:]]})')
 260      status, skin, phase = dump['status'], dump['skin'], state['phase']
 261      if status >= STATUS_CRITICAL or (phase == 'HOLD' and status > STATUS_RESUME):
 262          return 'HOLD', f'Android thermal status {status}: motors stopped until status <= {STATUS_RESUME}'
 263      note = (f'Android thermal status SEVERE ({status}): no optional Gemma work in the mission loop, nothing changed'
 264              if status == STATUS_SEVERE else '')
 265      # Adaptive rule copied from benchmark/campaign/phase23.py: switch() ('adaptive').
 266      elapsed = now - state['since']
 267      if phase == 'ACTIVE':
 268          if skin >= SKIN_PAUSE_C and elapsed >= MIN_ACTIVE_S:
 269              return 'PAUSE', f'{SKIN} {skin:.2f} >= {SKIN_PAUSE_C} after {elapsed:.0f} s active'
 270          return 'ACTIVE', note
 271      if skin <= SKIN_RESUME_C and elapsed >= MIN_PAUSE_S:
 272          return 'ACTIVE', f'{SKIN} {skin:.2f} <= {SKIN_RESUME_C} after {elapsed:.0f} s paused'
 273      return 'PAUSE', note
END
BEGIN heat_control.py (345 lines, excerpt lines 276-345)
 276  class HeatControl:
 277      """The mission's heat controller: monitor readings -> decide(), the phase state, and the thermal trace."""
 278  
 279      def __init__(self, monitor, trace_dir=TRACE_DIR, clock=time.monotonic):
 280          self.monitor, self.trace_dir, self.clock = monitor, trace_dir, clock
 281          self.state = {'phase': 'ACTIVE', 'since': None}
 282          self.note = ''
 283          self.distance = None
 284          self.trace_path = None
 285          self.lock = threading.Lock()
 286          monitor.on_dump = lambda d: self.event('dumpsys', d['error'], d)
 287  
 288      @property
 289      def phase(self):
 290          return self.state['phase']
 291  
 292      def now(self):
 293          return self.clock()
 294  
 295      def start(self):
 296          """Starts the monitor (raises if it cannot) and begins ACTIVE; call just before the camera starts."""
 297          self.trace_path = os.path.join(self.trace_dir, time.strftime('heat_trace_%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ.jsonl', time.gmtime()))
 298          try:
 299              os.makedirs(self.trace_dir, exist_ok=True)
 300          except OSError as e:
 301              print(f'[HEAT] trace folder failed: {e}')
 302          print(f'[HEAT] trace: {self.trace_path}')
 303          self.monitor.start()
 304          self.state = {'phase': 'ACTIVE', 'since': self.clock()}
 305          self.event('start')
 306  
 307      def stop(self):
 308          self.monitor.stop()
 309  
 310      def decide(self, distance=None):
 311          self.distance = distance
 312          target, reason = decide(self.clock(), self.monitor.readings(), self.state)
 313          if target == 'END':
 314              self.event('end', reason)
 315          elif target == self.phase and reason != self.note:
 316              self.note = reason
 317              if reason:
 318                  print(f'[HEAT] {reason}')
 319                  self.event('note', reason)
 320          return target, reason
 321  
 322      def enter(self, phase, since=None):
 323          """Phase change; since=None keeps the current phase start (PAUSE <-> HOLD)."""
 324          old = self.phase
 325          self.state = {'phase': phase, 'since': self.state['since'] if since is None else since}
 326          self.note = ''
 327          self.event('hold_start' if phase == 'HOLD' else 'hold_end' if old == 'HOLD' else 'phase_change')
 328  
 329      def summary(self):
 330          d = self.monitor.readings()['dump'] or {}
 331          return f"skin {d.get('skin')} degC status {d.get('status')}"
 332  
 333      def event(self, name, detail=None, dump=None):
 334          """One JSON line in the trace; a write failure is printed, never raised."""
 335          r = self.monitor.readings()
 336          d, fast = dump or r['dump'] or {}, r['fast'][-1] if r['fast'] else {}
 337          row = {'t_mono': self.clock(), 't_utc': time.strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ', time.gmtime()), 'event': name,
 338                 'phase': self.phase, 'status': d.get('status'), 'skin_c': d.get('skin'),
 339                 'battery_c': fast.get('bat_c'), 'cpu_max_c': fast.get('cpu_c'), 'distance_cm': self.distance,
 340                 'detail': detail}
 341          try:
 342              with self.lock, open(self.trace_path, 'a') as f:
 343                  f.write(json.dumps(row) + '\n')
 344          except (OSError, TypeError) as e:
 345              print(f'[HEAT] trace write failed: {e}')
END
BEGIN benchmark/mind_picker/b2_rules/b2.py (488 lines, excerpt lines 184-208)
 184  def preflight(post, s, blocks, info, tag):
 185      """prompts[(variant, order, id)] = (text, ids, {name: id}); every prompt rendered and tokenized, no /completion.
 186      Problems go to info['problems']."""
 187      prompts, sizes = {}, {}
 188      for v, order in blocks:
 189          for it in s['items']:
 190              names = offered(it, order)
 191              text, ids, bad = b0.render(post, messages(s, it, v, order))
 192              if v == 'V3':  # scored position checked with an empty reason; rechecked per item with the real one
 193                  t2 = text + ACTION
 194                  nid, why = first_ids(post, t2, b0.tokenize(post, t2), names, ' ')
 195              else:
 196                  nid, why = first_ids(post, text, ids, names)
 197              info['problems'] += [f'{tag} {v}-{order} item {it["id"]}: {w}' for w in why + ([bad] if bad else [])]
 198              prompts[v, order, it['id']] = (text, ids, nid)
 199              sizes.setdefault(v, []).append(len(ids))
 200              info.setdefault('name_ids', {}).setdefault('V3' if v == 'V3' else 'V1/V2', {}).update(nid)
 201              if tag == 'DEV' and it['id'] == SHOW_ITEM and order == 'FWD':
 202                  info[f'prompt_{v}_dev_item{SHOW_ITEM}'] = post('/detokenize', {'tokens': ids})['content']
 203      for v in sizes:
 204          info.setdefault('system_tokens', {})[v] = len(b0.tokenize(post, SYSTEM[v], special=False))
 205          info.setdefault('prompt_tokens', {})[f'{tag} {v}'] = (min(sizes[v]), max(sizes[v]))
 206          if max(sizes[v]) + CTX_MARGIN > CTX:
 207              info['problems'].append(f'{tag} {v}: longest prompt {max(sizes[v])} + {CTX_MARGIN} > {CTX}')
 208      return prompts
END
BEGIN benchmark/mind_picker/b2_rules/b2.py (488 lines, excerpt lines 211-238)
 211  def score(post, it, v, order, prompt):
 212      """One row: probabilities over the offered names (raw and renormalised), top, margin, counters; V3 adds the
 213      reason and its step-1 counters."""
 214      names, (text, ids, nid) = offered(it, order), prompt
 215      t0, row = time.perf_counter(), {'variant': v, 'order': order, 'id': it['id'], 'offered': names}
 216      if v == 'V3':
 217          r1 = post('/completion', {'prompt': ids, **b0.DECODE, **REASON, 'cache_prompt': True})
 218          reason = r1['content']
 219          t2 = text + reason + ACTION
 220          ids = b0.tokenize(post, t2)
 221          nid, why = first_ids(post, t2, ids, names, ' ')
 222          if why:
 223              raise RuntimeError(f'scored position after the reason: {why}')
 224          tm1 = r1.get('timings', {})
 225          row.update(reason=reason, reason_tokens=r1.get('tokens_predicted'), reason_stop=r1.get('stop_type'),
 226                     reason_prompt_n=tm1.get('prompt_n'), reason_cache_n=tm1.get('cache_n'))
 227      body = {'prompt': ids, **b0.DECODE, **b0.PICK, 'cache_prompt': True}
 228      res = post('/completion', body)
 229      raw, norm = b0.probs(res, {n: [nid[n]] for n in names})
 230      if norm is None:
 231          raise RuntimeError(f'item {it["id"]}: an offered name is outside the top {b0.PICK["n_probs"]}: {raw}')
 232      top = max(norm, key=norm.get)  # ties: first offered
 233      second = sorted(norm.values())[-2] if len(norm) > 1 else 0.0
 234      tm = res.get('timings', {})
 235      row.update(prompt_tokens=len(ids), prompt_ids_sha256=sha(json.dumps(ids)), prompt_n=tm.get('prompt_n'),
 236                 cache_n=tm.get('cache_n'), prompt_ms=tm.get('prompt_ms'), wall_s=round(time.perf_counter() - t0, 3),
 237                 probs_raw=raw, probs=norm, top=top, margin=norm[top] - second)
 238      return row
END

  21  def good(skin=30.0, battery=90, mono=0.0, mem=('runner',)):
  22      return {'utc': 'T', 'mono': mono, 'caps': {p: 2000000 for p in b4.POLICIES}, 'root_rc': 0, 'battery_rc': 0,
  23              'dump_rc': 0, 'skin': skin, 'status': 0, 'battery_pct': battery, 'battery_c': 30.0, 'charge_uah': 3000000,
  24              'voltage_uv': 4000000, **{f'mem_{n}': {'Pss': 1000, 'VmHWM': 2000, 'pids': [1], 'rc': 0} for n in mem}}
  25

  29              'samples': [{'mono': 0, 'hits': []}, {'mono': 30, 'hits': list(hits)}, {'mono': 60, 'hits': []}]}
  30  
  31  
  32  def record(block='P', rows=(), **kw):
  33      mem = ('runner', 'llama_server') if block in b4.SERVER_BLOCKS else ('runner',)
  34      rec = {'block': block, 'errors': [], 'cores_before': True, 'cores_after': True, 'agents_before': '',
  35             'agents_after': '', 'start': good(mono=0, mem=mem), 'end': good(mono=180, mem=mem), 'watch': watch(),
  36             'phases': [{'phase': block, 'overrun_s': 2.0}]}
  37      return {**rec, **kw}, list(rows)
  38

  41  CYCLE = {'kind': 'cycle', 'cores_ok': True}

  45      def __init__(self):
  46          self.t = 1000.0
  47  
  48      def __call__(self):
  49          return self.t
  50  
  51      def sleep(self, s):
  52          self.t += s
  53  
  54  
  55  class Clocked(unittest.TestCase):
  56      """b4.clock / b4.pause replaced by a fake clock that only operations advance."""
  57

  59          self.saved = b4.clock, b4.pause, b4.cores_ok
  60          self.c = FakeClock()
  61          b4.clock, b4.pause, b4.cores_ok = self.c, self.c.sleep, lambda: True

  63      def tearDown(self):
  64          b4.clock, b4.pause, b4.cores_ok = self.saved
